  // Apps Script kadang membalas 404 acak (~30% saat diukur) walau deployment sehat.
  // Tanpa percobaan ulang, cek ongkir gagal diam-diam dan pembeli mengira ongkir tidak ada.
  function ambilUlang(url, sisa) {
    return fetch(url).then(function (r) {
      if (r.ok) return r.json();
      if (sisa > 0) return new Promise(function (s) { setTimeout(s, 700); }).then(function () { return ambilUlang(url, sisa - 1); });
      throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status);
    });
  }

  componentDidMount() {
    // Keranjang tersimpan TIDAK boleh dipercaya apa adanya: bentuk item pernah
    // berubah beberapa kali, dan satu item cacat bisa mematikan halaman.
    // Semuanya disaring & dilengkapi di sini; kalau gagal, simpanan dibuang.
    try {
      const simpan = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("cbm_cart") || "null");
      const segar = simpan && Date.now() - (simpan.t || 0) < 7 * 864e5;
      const versiCocok = simpan && Number(simpan.v) === this.CART_V;

      if (segar && simpan.ship) {
        this.setState(st => ({ ship: { ...st.ship,
          q: String(simpan.ship.q || ""),
          tujuan: simpan.ship.tujuan || null,
          alamat: String(simpan.ship.alamat || "") } }));
      }

      const b = simpan && simpan.buyer;
      const buyer = b ? {
        name: String(b.name || ""), phone: String(b.phone || ""), email: String(b.email || "")
      } : null;

      const cart = (segar && versiCocok && Array.isArray(simpan.cart))
        ? simpan.cart.map(i => this.bersihkanItem(i)).filter(Boolean)
        : [];

      if (cart.length || buyer) this.setState({ cart, buyer: buyer || this.state.buyer });
      if (simpan && !versiCocok) localStorage.removeItem("cbm_cart");
    } catch (err) {
      try { localStorage.removeItem("cbm_cart"); } catch (e) {}
    }
    this.loadSheet();
    this.loadCurrencies();
    this.loadReviews();
    this.loadReviewChat();
    this.loadSocial();
    this.loadGaleri();
    this.loadGaleriCustom();
    this.catatStat("kunjungan", "-");
    this.muatGTM();
    this.muatGoogleTag();
    try {
      // Toko Indonesia: default selalu Indonesia. Inggris hanya kalau dipilih.
      if (localStorage.getItem("cbm_lang") === "en") this.setState({ lang: "en" });
    } catch (e) {}
    const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    ['gclid', 'gbraid', 'wbraid'].forEach(k => {
      const val = q.get(k);
      if (val) {
        try { localStorage.setItem('cbm_' + k, val); } catch(e) {}
      }
    });

    const ref = (q.get("review") || q.get("ulasan") || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (ref) {
      this.setState(s => ({ reviewOpen: true, rv: { ...s.rv, order: ref } }));
      this.prefillReview(ref);
    }
    // Halaman tujuan per produk untuk Google Merchant Center. Feed mengirim
    // ?produk=<slug>, dan halaman harus benar-benar menampilkan produk itu -
    // kalau tidak, itemnya ditolak Google.
    const slugMasuk = (q.get("produk") || q.get("product") || "").trim();
    if (slugMasuk) {
      this._deepProduk = slugMasuk;
      this.terapkanDeepProduk();
    }
    if (q.get("diag") === "1") setTimeout(() => this.jalankanDiagnosa(), 600);
    const pesananRef = (q.get("pesanan") || q.get("lacak") || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (location.hash === "#lacak" || pesananRef) {
      this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, open: true, id: pesananRef || s.trk.id } }));
      if (pesananRef) setTimeout(() => this.cariPesanan(), 400);
    }
    this.onHash = () => { if (location.hash === "#lacak") this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, open: true } })); };
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", this.onHash);
    this.preTimer = setTimeout(() => this.hidePreloader(), 4200);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", this.lbKey);
    if (history.state && history.state.cbmOverlay) this._sentinel = true;
    this.onPop = () => { this._sentinel = false; this.tutupTeratas(); };
    window.addEventListener("popstate", this.onPop);
  }

  componentDidUpdate() {
    this.syncOverlay();
    // Katalog dan ulasan datang asinkron; data terstruktur disuntikkan begitu
    // keduanya ada, sekali saja (dijaga _ldDone di dalam metodenya).
    if (this.state.rows && this.state.reviews) this.suntikProdukLD(this.daftarKatalog());
    // Produk dimuat dari spreadsheet secara asinkron, jadi deep link diterapkan
    // lagi begitu datanya tiba. Penjaganya adalah _deepProduk, bukan prevState:
    // runtime memanggil componentDidUpdate hanya dengan prevProps, jadi argumen
    // kedua selalu undefined. terapkanDeepProduk() mengosongkan _deepProduk
    // sebelum setState, jadi tidak ada pengulangan.
    if (this._deepProduk && Array.isArray(this.state.rows) && this.state.rows.length) {
      this.terapkanDeepProduk();
    }
  }

  /** Nama produk menjadi penggal alamat. Harus sama dengan slugProduk di konektor. */
  slugKe(nama) {
    return String(nama || "").toLowerCase().trim()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "").slice(0, 60);
  }

  /**
   * Menyaring koleksi ke satu produk sesuai ?produk=<slug>, lalu menggulirkan
   * halaman ke sana. Kalau slug tidak dikenali, halaman dibiarkan normal.
   */
  terapkanDeepProduk() {
    const slug = this._deepProduk;
    const rows = this.state.rows;
    if (!slug || !Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) return;

    const cocok = rows.find(r => this.slugKe(r.nama || r.name) === slug);
    this._deepProduk = null;
    if (!cocok) return;

    // Halaman detail dibuka langsung: pengunjung dari Google mendarat pada
    // produk yang dia klik. Kotak pencarian TIDAK diisi - kalau diisi, menutup
    // detail meninggalkan katalog yang tersaring satu produk, dan pengunjung
    // mengira itu seluruh koleksi kami.
    const n = this.normalize(cocok);
    if (this.state.detail !== n.name + "|" + n.kategori) this.trackFunnel("view_item");
    this.setState({ detail: n.name + "|" + n.kategori, detailImg: 0, detailQty: 1 });
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el = document.getElementById("koleksi");
      if (el) window.scrollTo({ top: el.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - 80 });
    }, 250);
  }

  componentWillUnmount() {
    clearTimeout(this.preTimer);
    clearTimeout(this.preTimer2);
    document.removeEventListener("keydown", this.lbKey);
    window.removeEventListener("hashchange", this.onHash);
  }

  hidePreloader() {
    if (this.state.leaving || !this.state.loading) return;
    clearTimeout(this.preTimer);
    this.setState({ leaving: true });
    this.preTimer2 = setTimeout(() => this.setState({ loading: false }), 520);
  }

  simpanKeranjang() {
    try {
      const s = this.state.ship;
      localStorage.setItem("cbm_cart", JSON.stringify({
        v: this.CART_V,
        cart: this.state.cart, buyer: this.state.buyer, t: Date.now(),
        ship: { q: s.q, tujuan: s.tujuan, alamat: s.alamat }
      }));
    } catch (err) {}
  }

  rBio = { name: React.createRef(), phone: React.createRef(), email: React.createRef() };

  overlayAda(s) {
    return !!(s.lb.open || s.modal || s.trk.open || s.reviewOpen || (s.chat && s.chat.open) || s.cartOpen);
  }

  /**
   * Satu langkah riwayat saja selama ADA panel terbuka.
   * Menutup bertahap (langkah 2 -> 1) ditangani di dalam aplikasi, lalu
   * penanda dipasang ulang di render berikutnya. Tidak pernah mundur berkali-kali.
   */
  syncBack() {
    if (this._inSync) return;
    this._inSync = true;
    try {
      const ada = this.overlayAda(this.state);
      if (ada && !this._sentinel) {
        try { history.pushState({ cbmOverlay: 1 }, ""); this._sentinel = true; } catch (e) {}
      } else if (!ada && this._sentinel) {
        this._sentinel = false;
        if (history.state && history.state.cbmOverlay) { try { history.back(); } catch (e) {} }
      }
    } finally { this._inSync = false; }
  }

  rSheet = React.createRef();

  sheetStart = e => {
    this._y0 = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    this._dy = 0;
    const el = this.rSheet.current;
    if (el) el.style.transition = "none";
  };

  sheetMove = e => {
    if (this._y0 == null) return;
    const y = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    this._dy = Math.max(0, y - this._y0);
    const el = this.rSheet.current;
    if (el) el.style.transform = "translateY(" + this._dy + "px)";
  };

  sheetEnd = () => {
    const el = this.rSheet.current;
    const tutup = this._dy > 80;
    if (el) {
      el.style.transition = "transform .26s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)";
      el.style.transform = tutup ? "translateY(110%)" : "translateY(0)";
    }
    this._y0 = null; this._dy = 0;
    if (tutup) setTimeout(() => {
      if (el) { el.style.transition = ""; el.style.transform = ""; }
      this.setState({ cartOpen: false });
    }, 200);
  };

  tutupTeratas() {
    const s = this.state;
    if (s.lb.open) return this.setState(st => ({ lb: { ...st.lb, open: false } }));
    if (s.detail) return this.tutupDetail();
    if (s.modal) return this.setState({ modal: null });
    if (s.chat && s.chat.open) return this.setState(st => ({ chat: { ...st.chat, open: false } }));
    if (s.reviewOpen) return this.setState({ reviewOpen: false });
    if (s.trk.open) return this.setState(st => ({ trk: { ...st.trk, open: false } }));
    if (s.cartOpen && s.cartStep === 2) return this.setState({ cartStep: 1 });
    if (s.cartOpen) return this.setState({ cartOpen: false });
  }

  warnaField(k) {
    const salah = this.state.bioErr && this.state.bioErr[k] && this.cekBiodata(this.state.buyer)[k];
    return salah ? { b: "#93B173", bg: "#FFF7F5" } : { b: "#DEE7CE", bg: "#ffffff" };
  }

  cekBiodata(b) {
    const e = {};
    if (!b.name.trim()) e.name = "Nama lengkap belum diisi.";
    if (!b.phone.trim()) e.phone = "No. HP / WhatsApp belum diisi.";
    else if (b.phone.replace(/[^0-9]/g, "").length < 9) e.phone = "No. HP belum lengkap.";
    if (!b.email.trim()) e.email = "Email belum diisi.";
    else if (!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(b.email.trim())) e.email = "Format email belum benar.";
    return e;
  }

  tandaiBiodata(err) {
    const urut = ["name", "phone", "email"];
    const pertama = urut.filter(k => err[k])[0];
    this.setState({ bioErr: err, hint: err[pertama] || "" });
    if (pertama && this.rBio[pertama] && this.rBio[pertama].current) {
      setTimeout(() => { try { this.rBio[pertama].current.focus(); } catch (e) {} }, 60);
    }
  }

  /** Dipanggil dari renderVals - satu-satunya titik yang pasti berjalan tiap render. */
  tick() {
    if (this._inTick) return;
    this._inTick = true;
    try {
      const s = this.state;

      const sig = JSON.stringify({
        c: s.cart, b: s.buyer, q: s.ship.q, t: s.ship.tujuan, a: s.ship.alamat
      });
      if (sig !== this._simpanSig) {
        const pertama = this._simpanSig === undefined;
        this._simpanSig = sig;
        if (!pertama) this.simpanKeranjang();
      }

      const n = s.chat ? s.chat.msgs.length : 0;
      if (this._chatN !== undefined && n !== this._chatN) this.scrollChat();
      this._chatN = n;

      if (this.state.lang === "en" && !this._trJadwal) {
        this._trJadwal = true;
        requestAnimationFrame(() => { this._trJadwal = false; try { this.terjemahkan(); } catch (e) {} });
      }

      const cek = (kunci, nilai, muat) => {
        const v = String(nilai || "");
        if (this[kunci] !== undefined && this[kunci] !== v) muat();
        this[kunci] = v;
      };
      cek("_pSheet", this.props.sheetUrl, () => this.loadSheet());
      cek("_pRev", this.props.reviewSheetUrl, () => this.loadReviews());
      cek("_pSoc", this.props.socialSheetUrl, () => this.loadSocial());
    } finally { this._inTick = false; }
  }

  /**
   * Alamat sematan resmi tiap platform - tanpa API key, tanpa token.
   * Instagram, TikTok, dan YouTube menyediakannya secara terbuka.
   */
  embedInfo(link) {
    const u = String(link || "").trim();
    if (!u) return null;

    // Instagram: /embed/captioned menyertakan caption asli dari unggahannya.
    let m = u.match(/instagram\.com\/(p|reel|reels|tv)\/([\w-]+)/i);
    if (m) {
      const jenis = /reel/i.test(m[1]) ? "reel" : "p";
      const tegak = jenis === "reel";
      return {
        url: "https://www.instagram.com/" + jenis + "/" + m[2] + "/embed/captioned",
        ratio: tegak ? "9 / 19.5" : "1 / 1.62",
        jenis: tegak ? "Reel" : "Unggahan"
      };
    }

    m = u.match(/tiktok\.com\/[^/]+\/video\/(\d+)/i);
    if (m) return { url: "https://www.tiktok.com/embed/v2/" + m[1] + "?music_info=0&description=0", ratio: "9 / 18.4", jenis: "Video" };

    const ytOpsi = id => "?mute=1&loop=1&playlist=" + id + "&rel=0&modestbranding=1&playsinline=1";
    m = u.match(/youtube\.com\/shorts\/([\w-]{6,})/i);
    if (m) return { url: "https://www.youtube.com/embed/" + m[1] + ytOpsi(m[1]), ratio: "9 / 16", jenis: "Shorts" };

    m = u.match(/(?:youtube\.com\/watch\?v=|youtu\.be\/)([\w-]{6,})/i);
    if (m) return { url: "https://www.youtube.com/embed/" + m[1] + ytOpsi(m[1]), ratio: "16 / 9", jenis: "Video" };

    return null;
  }

  /** Link video yang bisa diputar sendiri (mp4/webm atau file video di Drive). */
  videoLangsung(u) {
    const s = String(u || "").trim();
    if (!s) return "";
    if (/\.(mp4|webm|mov)(\?|$)/i.test(s)) return s;
    // Kolomnya sudah bernama "video", jadi link Drive apa pun di sini memang video.
    if (/google/.test(s)) {
      const id = this.driveId(s);
      if (id) return "https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=" + id;
    }
    return s;
  }

  /**
   * Ref iframe sematan yang identitasnya stabil per URL.
   *
   * Sebelumnya ref dibuat ulang sebagai arrow function baru pada setiap render,
   * sehingga React melepas lalu memasang ulang ref itu terus-menerus. Setiap
   * embed pihak ketiga (Instagram/TikTok/YouTube) memuat skripnya sendiri dan
   * mengirim postMessage tanpa henti, yang memicu proteksi kelebihan beban.
   *
   * Sekarang ref di-cache per URL dan src iframe hanya diisi satu kali, saat
   * kartunya benar-benar mendekati viewport.
   */
  embedRefFor(url) {
    this._embedRefs = this._embedRefs || {};
    if (!this._embedRefs[url]) {
      this._embedRefs[url] = el => {
        if (!el || el.dataset.embedDimuat === "1") return;
        el.dataset.embedTunda = url;
        this.pantauEmbed(el);
      };
    }
    return this._embedRefs[url];
  }

  pantauEmbed(el) {
    if (typeof IntersectionObserver !== "function") return this.muatEmbed(el);

    // Jaring pengaman: di host yang menampilkan halaman pada tinggi penuh,
    // viewport tidak pernah bergeser relatif terhadap kartu, jadi observer
    // tidak pernah terpicu. Muat langsung kalau kartunya sudah dekat, dan
    // pasang batas waktu supaya sematan tidak pernah tertinggal kosong.
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const tinggi = window.innerHeight || 800;
    if (r.top < tinggi * 3) return this.muatEmbed(el);
    setTimeout(() => this.muatEmbed(el), 2500);

    if (!this._embedObs) {
      this._embedObs = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(en => {
          if (!en.isIntersecting) return;
          this._embedObs.unobserve(en.target);
          this.muatEmbed(en.target);
        });
      }, { rootMargin: "300px 0px" });
    }
    this._embedObs.observe(el);
  }

  muatEmbed(el) {
    const url = el && el.dataset.embedTunda;
    if (!url || el.dataset.embedDimuat === "1") return;
    el.dataset.embedDimuat = "1";
    el.src = url;
  }

  /** URL video resmi. Data Sheet adalah input tidak tepercaya. */
  socialUrl(link, platform) {
    try {
      const u = new URL(String(link || "").trim());
      if (u.protocol !== "https:") return false;
      const host = u.hostname.toLowerCase().replace(/^www\./, "");
      const plat = String(platform || "").toLowerCase();
      if (plat.startsWith("instagram")) return host === "instagram.com" && /^\/(reel|reels|p|tv)\/[\w-]+\/?$/i.test(u.pathname);
      if (plat.startsWith("tiktok")) return host === "tiktok.com" && /^\/@[^/]+\/video\/\d+\/?$/i.test(u.pathname);
      return false;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  /** Baris sosmed bersih, unggulan/viral/terbaru, maksimal 12. */
  socialRows(pakaiFilter) {
    const aktif = this.state.socFilter || "Semua";
    return (this.state.social || [])
      .filter(r => !/profil|profile|akun/i.test(String(r.tipe || r.jenis || "")))
      .map(r => {
        const plat = String(r.platform || "Instagram").trim() || "Instagram";
        const viralRaw = String(r.viral_score ?? r.viral ?? "").trim();
        const viral = viralRaw !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(viralRaw)) ? Math.max(0, Number(viralRaw)) : null;
        return {
          raw: r, platform: plat,
          link: String(r.link || "").trim(),
          judul: String(r.judul || r.caption || r.keterangan || "").trim(),
          gambar: this.imageCandidates(String(r.gambar || r.foto || "").trim())[0] || "",
          alts: this.imageCandidates(String(r.gambar || r.foto || "").trim()).slice(1).join(" "),
          video: this.videoLangsung(r.video || r.mp4 || ""),
          info: this.embedInfo(r.link),
          featured: /^(ya|yes|true|1|unggulan|featured)$/i.test(String(r.featured || r.unggulan || "").trim()),
          viral,
          published: Date.parse(String(r.published_at || r.tanggal || r.date || "")) || 0,
          warna: this.WARNA_SOSMED[plat.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+.*$/, "")] || this.WARNA_SOSMED.lainnya
        };
      })
      .filter(x => this.socialUrl(x.link, x.platform))
      .filter(x => !pakaiFilter || aktif === "Semua" || x.platform === aktif)
      .sort((a, b) => Number(b.featured) - Number(a.featured) || (b.viral ?? -1) - (a.viral ?? -1) || b.published - a.published)
      .slice(0, 12);
  }

  IKON_SOSMED = {

  };

  ikonPlatform(nama) {
    const k = String(nama || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+.*$/, "");
    return this.IKON_SOSMED[k] || this.IKON_SOSMED.lainnya;
  }

  WARNA_SOSMED = {
    instagram: "#C1558B", tiktok: "#2B2B2B", facebook: "#3B5998", youtube: "#C13B3B",
    shopee: "#D96C3B", tokopedia: "#4C9A5E", whatsapp: "#3F9E6E", pinterest: "#C0392B",
    twitter: "#2C3327", x: "#2C3327", threads: "#2C3327", lainnya: "#77855F"
  };

  /** Naikkan angka ini setiap bentuk item keranjang berubah. */
  CART_V = 3;

  /**
   * Mengembalikan item keranjang yang dijamin lengkap, atau null kalau
   * datanya tidak layak dipakai.
   */
  bersihkanItem(i) {
    if (!i || typeof i !== "object") return null;
    const name = String(i.name || i.nama || "").trim();
    const price = Number(String(i.price).replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, ""));
    const qty = Math.max(1, Math.min(99, parseInt(i.qty, 10) || 1));
    if (!name || !isFinite(price) || price <= 0) return null;
    const kategori = String(i.kategori || "Lainnya");
    return {
      key: String(i.key || (name + "|" + kategori)),
      name, kategori, price, qty,
      desc: String(i.desc || ""),
      stock: String(i.stock || "Ready"),
      stockQty: Number.isFinite(Number(i.stockQty)) ? Number(i.stockQty) : null,
      payLink: /^https?:\/\//i.test(String(i.payLink || "")) ? String(i.payLink) : "",
      isCustom: !!i.isCustom,
      priceLabel: String(i.priceLabel || ("Rp" + new Intl.NumberFormat("id-ID").format(price))),
      photo: String(i.photo || ""),
      opts: Array.isArray(i.opts) ? i.opts.filter(x => typeof x === "string") : []
    };
  }

  loadCurrencies() {
    fetch("currencies.json", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then(r => r.json()).then(j => {
        const currencies = ((j && j.items) || []).filter(x => /^(IDR|SGD|MYR)$/.test(String(x.kode || "").toUpperCase()) && Number(x.kurs) > 0 && !/^(tidak|no|false|0)$/i.test(String(x.aktif || "ya")));
        this.setState({ currencies });
      }).catch(() => this.setState({ currencies: [] }));
  }

  currencyInfo(code) {
    const c = String(code || this.state.currency || "IDR").toUpperCase();
    if (c === "IDR") return { kode: "IDR", kurs: 1, locale: "id-ID", simbol: "Rp" };
    const row = (this.state.currencies || []).find(x => String(x.kode || "").toUpperCase() === c);
    if (!row || !(Number(row.kurs) > 0)) return this.currencyInfo("IDR");
    return { kode: c, kurs: Number(row.kurs), locale: c === "SGD" ? "en-SG" : "ms-MY", simbol: c === "SGD" ? "S$" : "RM" };
  }

  displayMoney(idr) {
    const c = this.currencyInfo(); const n = Number(idr || 0);
    if (!n) return "Harga menyusul";
    if (c.kode === "IDR") return "Rp" + Math.round(n).toLocaleString("id-ID");
    return c.simbol + (n / c.kurs).toLocaleString(c.locale, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
  }

  setCurrency = e => {
    const code = String(e && e.target && e.target.value || "IDR").toUpperCase();
    const valid = code === "IDR" || (this.state.currencies || []).some(x => String(x.kode || "").toUpperCase() === code);
    this.setState({ currency: valid ? code : "IDR" });
  };

  /* ---------- BAHASA ----------
     Indonesia adalah bahasa asli halaman ini: teksnya tertulis langsung di
     markup supaya tampil seketika. Bahasa Inggris diterapkan setelah halaman
     tampil, dengan menukar teks yang cocok di kamus. Cara ini tidak menunda
     tampilan sedetik pun, dan teks apa pun yang belum diterjemahkan tetap
     terbaca dalam bahasa Indonesia - bukan kotak kosong. */

  /** Teks yang dirakit dengan angka/nama produk - dicocokkan berpola. */
  polaEN(s) {
    let m;
    if ((m = s.match(/^Stok (\d+)$/))) return m[1] + " in stock";
    if ((m = s.match(/^Menampilkan (\d+) dari (\d+) produk$/))) return "Showing " + m[1] + " of " + m[2] + " products";
    if ((m = s.match(/^Berikutnya \((\d+)\)$/))) return "Next (" + m[1] + ")";
    if ((m = s.match(/^Halaman (\d+) \/ (\d+)$/))) return "Page " + m[1] + " / " + m[2];
    if ((m = s.match(/^(\d+) ulasan$/))) return m[1] + " reviews";
    if ((m = s.match(/^(\d+) tusukan × (\d+)/))) return s.replace("tusukan ×", "stitches ×");
    if ((m = s.match(/^Mulai dari (.+)$/))) return "From " + m[1];
    if ((m = s.match(/^Sisa (\d+)$/))) return m[1] + " left";
    if ((m = s.match(/^(\d+) foto · geser$/))) return m[1] + " photos · swipe";
    if ((m = s.match(/^Halaman (\d+) dari (\d+)$/))) return "Page " + m[1] + " of " + m[2];
    if ((m = s.match(/^(\d+) produk$/))) return m[1] + " products";
    if ((m = s.match(/^Lihat (\d+) layanan lain$/))) return "Show " + m[1] + " more services";
    return null;
  }

  terjemahkan() {
    if (this.state.lang !== "en") return;
    const K = this.KAMUS;
    const jalan = document.createTreeWalker(document.body, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
    const simpul = [];
    while (jalan.nextNode()) simpul.push(jalan.currentNode);
    simpul.forEach(n => {
      const s = n.nodeValue.trim();
      if (!s || s.length < 2) return;
      const t = K[s] || this.polaEN(s);
      if (t && t !== s) n.nodeValue = n.nodeValue.replace(s, t);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll("[placeholder],[aria-label],[title]").forEach(el => {
      ["placeholder", "aria-label", "title"].forEach(a => {
        const v = el.getAttribute(a);
        if (!v) return;
        const t = K[v.trim()];
        if (t && t !== v) el.setAttribute(a, t);
      });
    });
    if (K[document.title]) document.title = K[document.title];
    document.documentElement.lang = "en";
  }

  gantiBahasa = () => {
    const baru = this.state.lang === "en" ? "id" : "en";
    try { localStorage.setItem("cbm_lang", baru); } catch (e) {}
    // Kembali ke Indonesia paling bersih lewat muat ulang: teks aslinya
    // memang yang tertulis di markup.
    if (baru === "id") return location.reload();
    this.setState({ lang: "en" });
  };

  catatStat(jenis, nama) {
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return;
    this._stat = this._stat || {};
    const kunci = jenis + "|" + (nama || "");
    if (this._stat[kunci]) return;           // sekali saja per kunjungan
    this._stat[kunci] = true;
    try {
      fetch(api, {
        method: "POST", keepalive: true,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ type: "stat", jenis, nama: nama || "-" })
      }).catch(() => {});
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  loadGaleri() {
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return;
    fetch(api + (api.indexOf("?") > -1 ? "&" : "?") + "action=galeri")
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(j => {
        const items = (j && j.items) || [];
        if (items.length) this.setState({ galeri: items });
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }

  loadGaleriCustom() {
    // Sumber utama: berkas statis yang disinkronkan dari Sheet GaleriCustom.
    // Tidak bergantung pada konektor Apps Script supaya galeri tetap tampil.
    fetch("galeri-custom.json", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(j => {
        const items = (j && j.items) || [];
        this.setState({ galeriCustom: items });
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }

  loadSocial() {
    const url = this.csvUrl(this.props.socialSheetUrl);
    if (!url) return this.setState({ social: null });
    fetch(url)
      .then(r => r.text())
      .then(t => {
        const rows = this.parseCsvRaw(t).filter(r => {
          const tampil = String(r.tampil || r.publish || "ya").trim().toLowerCase();
          return !/^(tidak|no|false|0)$/.test(tampil) && String(r.link || "").trim();
        });
        this.setState({ social: rows.length ? rows : null });
      })
      .catch(() => this.setState({ social: null }));
  }

  loadReviewChat() {
    // Sumber utama: berkas statis yang disinkronkan dari Sheet ReviewChat.
    fetch("review-chat.json", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(j => { const items = (j && Array.isArray(j.items)) ? j.items : []; this.setState({ reviewChat: items }); })
      .catch(() => this.setState({ reviewChat: [] }));
  }

  loadReviews() {
    const url = this.csvUrl(this.props.reviewSheetUrl);
    if (!url) return this.setState({ reviews: null });
    fetch(url)
      .then(r => r.text())
      .then(t => {
        const rows = this.parseCsvRaw(t).filter(r => {
          const show = String(r.tampil || r.publish || r.status || "").trim().toLowerCase();
          return /^(ya|yes|true|tampil|1|ok|setuju)$/.test(show) && String(r.ulasan || r.review || "").trim();
        });
        this.setState({ reviews: rows.length ? rows : null });
      })
      .catch(() => this.setState({ reviews: null }));
  }

  prefillReview(ref) {
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return;
    fetch(api + (api.includes("?") ? "&" : "?") + "action=order&id=" + encodeURIComponent(ref))
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(res => {
        if (!res || !res.ok) return;
        this.setState(s => ({
          rv: {
            ...s.rv,
            name: s.rv.name || res.nama || "",
            product: s.rv.product || res.produk || ""
          }
        }));
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }

  submitReview = () => {
    const { rv } = this.state;
    if (!rv.name.trim()) return this.setState({ reviewHint: "Isi nama kamu dulu ya." });
    if (rv.text.trim().length < 12) return this.setState({ reviewHint: "Ceritakan sedikit lebih panjang (minimal 12 karakter)." });
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) {
      const msg = ["Halo, saya mau kirim ulasan:", "", "Nama : " + rv.name.trim(),
        "Penilaian : " + rv.rating + "/5", rv.product.trim() ? "Produk : " + rv.product.trim() : "",
        rv.order.trim() ? "No. Pesanan : " + rv.order.trim() : "", "", rv.text.trim()]
        .filter(Boolean).join("\n");
      this.trackWhatsapp();
      window.open(this.waText(msg), "_blank");
      return this.setState({ reviewSent: true, reviewHint: "" });
    }
    this.setState({ rvBusy: true, reviewHint: "" });
    fetch(api, {
      method: "POST",
      mode: "no-cors",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        type: "review",
        visitor: this.aiVisitorId(),
        orderId: rv.order.trim().toUpperCase(),
        nama: rv.name.trim(),
        rating: rv.rating,
        produk: rv.product.trim(),
        ulasan: rv.text.trim()
      })
    })
      .then(() => this.setState({ rvBusy: false, reviewSent: true }))
      .catch(() => this.setState({ rvBusy: false, reviewSent: true }));
  };

  patRef = React.createRef();

  onPatFile = e => {
    const file = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        this.patImg = img;
        this.setState(s => ({ pat: { ...s.pat, src: reader.result, ready: true } }), () => this.drawPattern());
      };
      img.src = reader.result;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  setPat(k, v) {
    this.setState(s => ({ pat: { ...s.pat, [k]: v } }), () => this.drawPattern());
  }

  drawPattern() {
    const img = this.patImg;
    const cv = this.patRef.current;
    if (!img || !cv) return;
    const { grid, colors } = this.state.pat;

    // 1. ambil sampel kecil sebesar jumlah tusukan
    const side = Math.min(img.width, img.height);
    const sx = (img.width - side) / 2;
    const sy = (img.height - side) / 2;
    const tmp = document.createElement("canvas");
    tmp.width = grid; tmp.height = grid;
    const tc = tmp.getContext("2d");
    tc.drawImage(img, sx, sy, side, side, 0, 0, grid, grid);
    const px = tc.getImageData(0, 0, grid, grid).data;

    // 2. kurangi jadi palet warna benang
    const bucket = {};
    for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 4) {
      const key = ((px[i] >> 4) << 8) | ((px[i + 1] >> 4) << 4) | (px[i + 2] >> 4);
      if (!bucket[key]) bucket[key] = { n: 0, r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 };
      const b = bucket[key];
      b.n++; b.r += px[i]; b.g += px[i + 1]; b.b += px[i + 2];
    }
    const palette = Object.keys(bucket)
      .map(k => bucket[k])
      .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)
      .slice(0, colors)
      .map(b => ({ r: Math.round(b.r / b.n), g: Math.round(b.g / b.n), b: Math.round(b.b / b.n), n: 0 }));
    if (!palette.length) return;

    const near = (r, g, b) => {
      let best = 0, bd = Infinity;
      for (let i = 0; i < palette.length; i++) {
        const p = palette[i];
        const d = (p.r - r) ** 2 + (p.g - g) ** 2 + (p.b - b) ** 2;
        if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = i; }
      }
      return best;
    };

    // 3. gambar tusukan
    const S = Math.max(7, Math.round(680 / grid));
    cv.width = grid * S;
    cv.height = grid * S;
    const ctx = cv.getContext("2d");
    ctx.fillStyle = "#FAFBF4";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);

    for (let y = 0; y < grid; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < grid; x++) {
        const i = (y * grid + x) * 4;
        const idx = near(px[i], px[i + 1], px[i + 2]);
        const p = palette[idx];
        p.n++;
        const X = x * S, Y = y * S;
        const rr = Math.max(1.5, S * 0.28);
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgb(" + p.r + "," + p.g + "," + p.b + ")";
        ctx.beginPath();
        if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(X + 0.6, Y + 0.6, S - 1.2, S - 1.2, rr);
        else ctx.rect(X + 0.6, Y + 0.6, S - 1.2, S - 1.2);
        ctx.fill();
        // sorot & bayangan tusukan "V"
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,.30)";
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.8, S * 0.11);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(X + S * 0.26, Y + S * 0.28);
        ctx.lineTo(X + S * 0.5, Y + S * 0.74);
        ctx.lineTo(X + S * 0.74, Y + S * 0.28);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(61,44,37,.16)";
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, S * 0.07);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(X + S * 0.5, Y + S * 0.76);
        ctx.lineTo(X + S * 0.74, Y + S * 0.3);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    }

    const hex = p => "#" + [p.r, p.g, p.b].map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    const total = grid * grid;
    const yarns = palette
      .filter(p => p.n > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)
      .map(p => ({ hex: hex(p), label: hex(p).toUpperCase() + " · " + Math.round((p.n / total) * 100) + "%" }));

    const cmPerStitch = 0.7;
    const size = Math.round(grid * cmPerStitch);
    this.setState(s => ({
      pat: {
        ...s.pat,
        yarns,
        stats: grid + " × " + grid + " tusukan (" + total.toLocaleString("id-ID") + " tusuk) · " +
          yarns.length + " warna benang · perkiraan ukuran jadi ± " + size + " × " + size + " cm"
      }
    }));
  }

  aiVisitorId() {
    let id = "";
    try {
      id = localStorage.getItem("cbm_visitor") || "";
      if (!id) { id = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10); localStorage.setItem("cbm_visitor", id); }
    } catch (err) { id = "anon"; }
    return id;
  }

  runAiPreview = () => {
    const img = this.patImg;
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!img) return this.setState(s => ({ ai: { ...s.ai, err: "Unggah fotonya dulu ya." } }));
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return this.setState(s => ({ ai: { ...s.ai, err: "Konektor belum tersambung." } }));
    if (this.state.ai.busy) return;

    // perkecil foto supaya cepat & hemat kuota
    const side = Math.min(img.width, img.height);
    const N = 768;
    const cv = document.createElement("canvas");
    cv.width = N; cv.height = N;
    cv.getContext("2d").drawImage(img, (img.width - side) / 2, (img.height - side) / 2, side, side, 0, 0, N, N);
    const b64 = cv.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.86).split(",")[1];

    this.setState(s => ({ ai: { ...s.ai, busy: true, err: "", img: "" } }));
    const timeout = new Promise((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(new Error("timeout")), 90000));
    Promise.race([
      fetch(api, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ type: "ai-crochet", gaya: this.state.ai.gaya, mime: "image/jpeg", data: b64, visitor: this.aiVisitorId() })
      }).then(r => r.json()),
      timeout
    ])
      .then(res => {
        if (!res || !res.ok || !res.data) throw new Error((res && res.message) || "gagal");
        this.setState(s => ({ ai: { ...s.ai, busy: false, img: "data:" + (res.mime || "image/png") + ";base64," + res.data } }));
      })
      .catch(err => this.setState(s => ({
        ai: { ...s.ai, busy: false, err: String(err.message || "").includes("timeout")
          ? "Prosesnya terlalu lama. Coba lagi, atau langsung chat WhatsApp kami."
          : (String(err.message) || "Pratinjau gagal dibuat.") }
      })));
  };

  downloadAi = () => {
    if (!this.state.ai.img) return;
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = this.state.ai.img;
    a.download = "pratinjau-boneka-rajut.png";
    a.click();
  };

  cleanReply(t) {
    return String(t || "")
      // buang placeholder seperti [Link WhatsApp] / [link di bawah]
      .replace(/\[[^\]]*(link|wa|whatsapp|url|tautan)[^\]]*\]/gi, "tombol WhatsApp di bawah")
      .replace(/\(\s*(link|url|tautan)[^)]*\)/gi, "")
      .replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, " ")
      .trim();
  }

  linkifyReply(t) {
    const clean = this.cleanReply(t);
    const rx = /(https:\/\/machelcrochet\.my\.id\/\?produk=[a-z0-9-]+)/gi;
    return clean.split(rx).map((p, i) => /^https:\/\/machelcrochet\.my\.id\/\?produk=[a-z0-9-]+$/i.test(p)
      ? React.createElement("a", {
          key: i, href: p, target: "_blank", rel: "noopener noreferrer",
          style: { color: "#2F7A52", fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "underline", wordBreak: "break-all" }
        }, p)
      : p);
  }

  TAHAPAN = [
    { key: "MENUNGGU BAYAR", label: "Menunggu pembayaran", desc: "Pesanan tercatat. Kami menunggu pembayaranmu masuk." },
    { key: "LUNAS", label: "Pembayaran diterima", desc: "Pembayaran terkonfirmasi. Pesanan masuk antrean pengerjaan." },
    { key: "DIRAJUT", label: "Sedang dirajut", desc: "Boneka dikerjakan satu per satu dengan tangan." },
    { key: "SIAP KIRIM", label: "Selesai & dikemas", desc: "Rajutan selesai, dirapikan dan dibungkus rapi." },
    { key: "DIKIRIM", label: "Dalam perjalanan", desc: "Sudah diserahkan ke kurir menuju alamatmu." },
    { key: "SELESAI", label: "Sampai tujuan", desc: "Pesanan diterima. Terima kasih sudah memilih handmade." }
  ];

  KURIR_URL = {
    jne: "https://www.jne.co.id/tracking-package",
    jnt: "https://www.jet.co.id/track",
