  openTrack = e => {
    if (e && e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
    this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, open: true } }));
    if (location.hash !== "#lacak") history.pushState(null, "", "#lacak");
  };

  closeTrack = () => {
    this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, open: false } }));
    if (location.hash === "#lacak") history.pushState(null, "", location.pathname + location.search);
  };

  setTrkId = e => {
    const v = e.target.value;
    this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, id: v } }));
  };

  trkKey = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") this.cariPesanan(); };

  copyResi = () => {
    const r = this.state.trk.data && this.state.trk.data.resi;
    if (!r) return;
    const done = () => {
      this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, copied: true } }));
      setTimeout(() => this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, copied: false } })), 1800);
    };
    if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(r).then(done, () => {});
    else done();
  };

  cariPesanan = () => {
    const id = this.state.trk.id.trim().toUpperCase();
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!id) return this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, err: "Masukkan nomor pesanan dulu ya." } }));
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api))
      return this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, err: "Pelacakan belum aktif. Chat WhatsApp kami untuk cek pesanan." } }));

    this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, busy: true, err: "", data: null } }));
    fetch(api + (api.indexOf("?") > -1 ? "&" : "?") + "action=track&id=" + encodeURIComponent(id))
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(res => {
        if (res && res.ok) this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, busy: false, data: res, err: "" } }));
        else this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, busy: false, data: null, err: (res && res.message) || "Pesanan tidak ditemukan." } }));
      })
      .catch(() => this.setState(s => ({ trk: { ...s.trk, busy: false, err: "Koneksi bermasalah. Coba lagi sebentar." } })));
  };

  openLightbox(imgs, i, title) {
    if (title) this.catatStat("lihat_produk", title);
    this.setState({ lb: { open: true, imgs, i, title } });
  }

  closeLightbox = () => this.setState(s => ({ lb: { ...s.lb, open: false } }));

  stepLightbox = d => {
    this.setState(s => {
      const n = s.lb.imgs.length;
      if (!n) return null;
      return { lb: { ...s.lb, i: (s.lb.i + d + n) % n } };
    });
  };

  lbKey = e => {
    if (this.state.detail && !this.state.lb.open) {
      if (e.key === "Escape") this.tutupDetail();
      if (e.key === "ArrowRight") this.setState(s => ({ detailImg: (s.detailImg || 0) + 1 }));
      if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") this.setState(s => ({ detailImg: Math.max(0, (s.detailImg || 0) - 1) }));
      return;
    }
    if (!this.state.lb.open) return;
    if (e.key === "Escape") this.closeLightbox();
    if (e.key === "ArrowRight") this.stepLightbox(1);
    if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") this.stepLightbox(-1);
  };

  setChatEl = el => { if (el) this.chatEl = el; };

  scrollChat() {
    const el = this.chatEl || document.querySelector("[data-chat-scroll]");
    if (el) el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;
  }

  toggleChat = () => {
    this.setState(s => {
      const open = !s.chat.open;
      const msgs = s.chat.msgs.length ? s.chat.msgs : [{
        role: "bot",
        text: "Hai! Aku Asisten Machel 🧶 Mau tanya soal produk, harga, atau request custom? Tanya saja di sini."
      }];
      return { chat: { ...s.chat, open, msgs } };
    }, () => setTimeout(() => this.scrollChat(), 60));
  };

  setChatText = e => {
    const v = e.target.value;
    this.setState(s => ({ chat: { ...s.chat, text: v } }));
  };

  chatKey = e => { if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); this.sendChat(); } };

  askChat = q => { this.setState(s => ({ chat: { ...s.chat, text: q } }), () => this.sendChat()); };

  sendChat = () => {
    const q = this.state.chat.text.trim();
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!q || this.state.chat.busy) return;
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return;

    const history = this.state.chat.msgs.map(m => ({ role: m.role === "user" ? "user" : "bot", text: m.text }));
    this.setState(s => ({
      chat: { ...s.chat, busy: true, text: "", msgs: [...s.chat.msgs, { role: "user", text: q }] }
    }), () => this.scrollChat());

    const timeout = new Promise((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(new Error("timeout")), 30000));
    Promise.race([
      fetch(api, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ type: "chat", message: q, history, visitor: this.aiVisitorId() })
      }).then(r => r.json()),
      timeout
    ])
      .then(res => {
        const umum = "Maaf, aku belum bisa menjawab itu. Chat WhatsApp kami ya, dijawab langsung oleh Machel.";
        // pesan server hanya dipakai kalau memang ditulis untuk pelanggan
        const ramah = res && res.message && /istirahat|sibuk|WhatsApp|dimatikan/i.test(res.message)
          && !/payload|key|script|error|undefined|quota exceeded/i.test(res.message);
        const reply = (res && res.ok && res.reply) || (ramah ? res.message : umum);
        this.setState(s => ({ chat: { ...s.chat, busy: false, msgs: [...s.chat.msgs, { role: "bot", text: reply }] } }),
          () => this.scrollChat());
      })
      .catch(() => {
        this.setState(s => ({
          chat: { ...s.chat, busy: false, msgs: [...s.chat.msgs, { role: "bot", text: "Koneksinya lambat. Coba lagi, atau chat WhatsApp kami ya." }] }
        }), () => this.scrollChat());
      });
  };

  downloadPattern = () => {
    const cv = this.patRef.current;
    if (!cv) return;
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = cv.toDataURL("image/png");
    a.download = "pola-rajut-" + this.state.pat.grid + "x" + this.state.pat.grid + ".png";
    a.click();
  };

  setRv(k, v) { this.setState(s => ({ rv: { ...s.rv, [k]: v }, reviewHint: "" })); }

  productThumbs() {
    const map = {};
    try {
      (this.state.rows || []).forEach(r => {
        const nama = String((r && r.nama) || "").trim();
        if (!nama) return;
        const g = r.gambar;
        const list = Array.isArray(g) ? g : (g ? [g] : []);
        const img = list.map(String).find(u => u.trim());
        if (img) map[nama.toLowerCase()] = this.imageCandidates(img.trim())[0];
      });
    } catch (err) { return {}; }
    return map;
  }

  matchThumb(produk, thumbs) {
    if (!thumbs) return "";
    const p = String(produk || "").toLowerCase();
    if (!p) return "";
    if (thumbs[p]) return thumbs[p];
    const hit = Object.keys(thumbs).find(k => p.includes(k));
    return hit ? thumbs[hit] : "";
  }

  reviewVals() {
    const rows = this.state.reviews;
    if (!rows) {
      return {
        reviewChatAda: !!(this.state.reviewChat && this.state.reviewChat.length),
        reviewChat: (this.state.reviewChat || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>(parseInt(a.urutan,10)||99)-(parseInt(b.urutan,10)||99)).map((r,i)=>({
          gambar: this.imageCandidates(String(r.gambar || "").trim())[0] || "",
          alt: String(r.alt || "Screenshot review pelanggan yang telah disensor"),
          platform: String(r.platform || "Chat"),
          zoom: () => this.openLightbox([{ url: this.imageCandidates(String(r.gambar || "").trim())[0] || "", alt: String(r.alt || "Screenshot review pelanggan") }], 0, String(r.platform || "Review pelanggan"))
        })).filter(r=>r.gambar),
        reviewCards: [
          { quote: "Lucu banget dan hasilnya sesuai request! Detailnya juga rapi.", by: "Placeholder ulasan", stars: "★★★★★", verified: false, meta: "", date: "", hasProduct: false, product: "", thumbEl: null, hasThumb: false, noThumb: false },
          { quote: "Awalnya cuma kirim foto referensi, ternyata hasil rajutannya malah lebih gemas dari bayangan saya.", by: "Placeholder ulasan", stars: "★★★★★", verified: false, meta: "", date: "", hasProduct: false, product: "", thumbEl: null, hasThumb: false, noThumb: false },
          { quote: "Dikemas rapi, dikirim cepat, dan yang dikasih hadiah langsung senang.", by: "Placeholder ulasan", stars: "★★★★★", verified: false, meta: "", date: "", hasProduct: false, product: "", thumbEl: null, hasThumb: false, noThumb: false }
        ],
        hasReviews: false, noReviews: true, rvHasPages: false, rvPages: [],
        reviewAvg: "", reviewAvgStars: "", reviewCountLabel: ""
      };
    }
    const thumbs = this.productThumbs();
    const stars = n => "★★★★★".slice(0, n) + "☆☆☆☆☆".slice(0, 5 - n);
    const cards = rows.map(r => {
      const n = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, parseInt(String(r.rating).replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""), 10) || 5));
      const produk = String(r.produk || r.product || "").trim();
      const waktu = String(r.waktu || r.tanggal || "").trim().split(" ")[0];
      const verified = /^cbm-/i.test(String(r.order_id || r.orderid || "").trim());
      const thumb = (verified && this.matchThumb(produk, thumbs)) || "";
      return {
        quote: String(r.ulasan || r.review || "").trim(),
        by: String(r.nama || r.name || "Pelanggan").trim(),
        stars: stars(n),
        rating: n,
        verified,
        hasProduct: verified && !!produk,
        product: produk,
        thumbEl: thumb
          ? React.createElement("img", { src: thumb, alt: produk, style: { width: "100%", height: "100%", objectFit: "cover" } })
          : null,
        hasThumb: !!thumb,
        noThumb: verified && !!produk && !thumb,
        date: waktu,
        meta: [produk, waktu].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")
      };
    });
    const avg = cards.reduce((t, c) => t + c.rating, 0) / cards.length;

    const per = 6;
    const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(cards.length / per));
    const page = Math.min(Math.max(1, this.state.reviewPage), totalPages);
    const goPage = n => {
      const p = Math.min(Math.max(1, n), totalPages);
      if (p === page) return;
      this.setState({ reviewPage: p }, () => {
        const el = document.getElementById("testimoni");
        if (el) window.scrollTo({ top: el.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - 90, behavior: "smooth" });
      });
    };
    const pages = Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => {
      const n = i + 1;
      const on = n === page;
      return {
        label: String(n), go: () => goPage(n),
        bg: on ? "#7E9B62" : "#fff",
        color: on ? "#fff" : "#5A664D",
        border: on ? "#7E9B62" : "#DEE7CE"
      };
    });

    return {
      reviewChatAda: !!(this.state.reviewChat && this.state.reviewChat.length),
        reviewChat: (this.state.reviewChat || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>(parseInt(a.urutan,10)||99)-(parseInt(b.urutan,10)||99)).map((r,i)=>({
          gambar: this.imageCandidates(String(r.gambar || "").trim())[0] || "",
          alt: String(r.alt || "Screenshot review pelanggan yang telah disensor"),
          platform: String(r.platform || "Chat"),
          zoom: () => this.openLightbox([{ url: this.imageCandidates(String(r.gambar || "").trim())[0] || "", alt: String(r.alt || "Screenshot review pelanggan") }], 0, String(r.platform || "Review pelanggan"))
        })).filter(r=>r.gambar),
        reviewCards: cards.slice((page - 1) * per, page * per),
      hasReviews: true,
      noReviews: false,
      rvHasPages: totalPages > 1,
      rvPages: pages,
      rvPrev: () => goPage(page - 1),
      rvNext: () => goPage(page + 1),
      rvOnFirst: page === 1,
      rvOnLast: page === totalPages,
      rvPrevColor: page === 1 ? "#B7C3AA" : "#5A664D",
      rvPrevCursor: page === 1 ? "default" : "pointer",
      rvNextBg: page === totalPages ? "#fff" : "#E7EEDA",
      rvNextColor: page === totalPages ? "#B7C3AA" : "#55763F",
      rvNextCursor: page === totalPages ? "default" : "pointer",
      rvPageLabel: "Halaman " + page + " dari " + totalPages + " · " + cards.length + " ulasan",
      reviewAvg: (Math.round(avg * 10) / 10).toFixed(1).replace(".", ","),
      reviewAvgStars: stars(Math.round(avg)),
      reviewCountLabel: cards.length + " ulasan"
    };
  }

  csvUrl(u) {
    const s = String(u || "").trim();
    if (!s) return "";
    if (/output=csv|out:csv/.test(s)) return s;
    const m = s.match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9-_]+)/);
    if (!m) return "";
    const gid = (s.match(/[#&?]gid=(\d+)/) || [])[1];
    return `https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/${m[1]}/gviz/tq?tqx=out:csv${gid ? "&gid=" + gid : ""}`;
  }

  parseCsvRaw(text) {
    const rows = [];
    let row = [], cell = "", q = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
      const c = text[i];
      if (q) {
        if (c === '"' && text[i + 1] === '"') { cell += '"'; i++; }
        else if (c === '"') q = false;
        else cell += c;
      } else if (c === '"') q = true;
      else if (c === ",") { row.push(cell); cell = ""; }
      else if (c === "\n") { row.push(cell); rows.push(row); row = []; cell = ""; }
      else if (c !== "\r") cell += c;
    }
    if (cell || row.length) { row.push(cell); rows.push(row); }
    if (rows.length < 2) return [];
    const head = rows[0].map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase());
    return rows.slice(1)
      .map(r => { const o = {}; head.forEach((h, i) => (o[h] = r[i] || "")); return o; })
      .filter(o => Object.keys(o).some(k => String(o[k]).trim()));
  }

  parseCsv(text) {
    const pick = (o, ...keys) => { for (const k of keys) if (o[k]) return String(o[k]).trim(); return ""; };
    return this.parseCsvRaw(text)
      .map(o => ({
        kategori: pick(o, "kategori", "category") || "Koleksi",
        nama: pick(o, "nama", "name", "produk", "product"),
        deskripsi: pick(o, "deskripsi", "description", "desc", "keterangan"),
        harga: pick(o, "harga", "price"),
        stok: pick(o, "stok", "stock", "status", "ketersediaan"),
        link_bayar: pick(o, "link_bayar", "link bayar", "payment", "link pembayaran", "bayar"),
        gambar: Object.keys(o)
          .filter(k => /^(gambar|image|foto|url|link foto)/.test(k))
          .map(k => String(o[k] || "").trim())
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(","),
        custom: pick(o, "custom", "bisa custom")
      }))
      .filter(o => o.nama);
  }

  loadSheet() {
    const url = this.csvUrl(this.props.sheetUrl);
    if (!url) {
      setTimeout(() => this.hidePreloader(), 1100);
      return this.setState({ rows: null, sheetStatus: "fallback" });
    }
    this.setState({ sheetStatus: "loading" });
    const awal = window.__cbmProductFetch;
    window.__cbmProductFetch = null;
    const permintaan = awal
      ? awal.then(t => t === null ? fetch(url).then(r => r.text()) : t)
      : fetch(url).then(r => r.text());
    permintaan.then(t => {
        const rows = this.parseCsv(t);
        this.setState({ rows: rows.length ? rows : null, sheetStatus: rows.length ? "ok" : "fallback" });
        setTimeout(() => this.hidePreloader(), 100);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        this.setState({ rows: null, sheetStatus: "error" });
        setTimeout(() => this.hidePreloader(), 700);
      });
  }

  driveId(u) {
    const m = String(u).match(/(?:\/file\/d\/|[?&]id=|\/d\/|drive\.google\.com\/uc\?[^#]*id=)([\w-]{20,})/)
      || String(u).match(/googleusercontent\.com\/d\/([\w-]{20,})/);
    return m ? m[1] : null;
  }

  imageCandidates(u) {
    const id = /google/.test(u) ? this.driveId(u) : null;
    if (!id) return [u];
    return [
      "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/" + id + "=w1200",
      "https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=" + id + "&sz=w1200",
      "https://drive.google.com/uc?export=view&id=" + id
    ];
  }

  imageUrls(raw) {
    return String(raw || "")
      .split(/[,\n|;]+/)
      .map(s => s.trim())
      .filter(s => /^https?:\/\//i.test(s) || s.startsWith("./") || s.startsWith("assets/"))
      .map(u => this.imageCandidates(u));
  }

  normalize(r) {
    const digits = parseInt(String(r.harga).replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""), 10) || 0;
    const stokRaw = String(r.stok || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    const numMatch = stokRaw.match(/^-?\d+/);
    const qty = numMatch ? parseInt(numMatch[0], 10) : null;
    const state = (qty !== null)
      ? (qty <= 0 ? "Habis" : "Ready")
      : (/habis|sold|kosong/.test(stokRaw) ? "Habis"
        : /pre|po\b|inden/.test(stokRaw) ? "Pre-order" : "Ready");
    return {
      kategori: r.kategori || "Koleksi",
      name: r.nama,
      desc: r.deskripsi,
      price: digits,
      priceLabel: digits ? `Rp${digits.toLocaleString("id-ID")}` : (String(r.harga).trim() || "Harga menyusul"),
      priceRaw: digits,
      stock: state,
      qty: qty,
      payLink: /^https?:\/\//i.test(String(r.link_bayar || "").trim()) ? String(r.link_bayar).trim() : "",
      stockNote: state === "Ready" ? "Siap kirim" : state === "Pre-order" ? "Dibuat setelah order" : "Sementara kosong",
      images: this.imageUrls(r.gambar),
      isCustom: /ya|yes|true|1/i.test(String(r.custom || ""))
    };
  }

  pulseGrid() {
    const g = document.querySelector(".cgrid");
    if (!g) return;
    g.classList.remove("anim");
    void g.offsetWidth;
    g.classList.add("anim");
  }

  slide(e, dir) {
    const wrap = e.currentTarget.parentElement;
    const t = wrap && wrap.querySelector("[data-track]");
    if (!t) return;
    const w = t.clientWidth || 1;
    const last = Math.max(0, Math.round(t.scrollWidth / w) - 1);
    const next = Math.min(last, Math.max(0, Math.round(t.scrollLeft / w) + dir));
    t.style.scrollSnapType = "none";
    t.scrollTo({ left: next * w, behavior: "smooth" });
    clearTimeout(t._snapTimer);
    t._snapTimer = setTimeout(() => { t.style.scrollSnapType = "x mandatory"; }, 450);
  }

  trackFunnel(event, paymentMethod) {
    try {
      if (!["view_item", "add_to_cart", "view_cart", "begin_checkout", "select_payment_method", "open_whatsapp", "open_payment_link"].includes(event)) return;
      const payload = { event };
      if (event === "select_payment_method") {
        if (!["whatsapp", "payment_link"].includes(paymentMethod)) return;
        payload.payment_method = paymentMethod;
      }
      window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
      window.dataLayer.push(payload);
    } catch (err) { /* Metrics must never interrupt checkout. */ }
  }

  trackWhatsapp = () => { this.trackFunnel("open_whatsapp"); };
  trackPaymentLink = () => { this.trackFunnel("open_payment_link"); };

  openModal(item) {
    if (item && this.state.modal !== item) this.trackFunnel("view_item");
    this.catatStat("lihat_produk", item && item.nama);
    this.setState({ modal: item, opt: { tema: "", kartu: false, pesanKartu: "", catatan: "", qty: 1, refName: "", refPreview: "" } });
  }

  setOpt(key, value) { this.setState(s => ({ opt: { ...s.opt, [key]: value } })); }

  handleRefFile = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => this.setOpt("refPreview", reader.result);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    this.setOpt("refName", file.name);
  };

  addConfigured = () => {
    if (this.LIBUR) return this.setState({ hint: this.LIBUR_MSG });
    const m = this.state.modal;
    if (!m) return;
    const o = this.state.opt;
    const opts = [];
    if (o.tema.trim()) opts.push(`Tema/karakter: ${o.tema.trim()}`);
    if (o.kartu) opts.push(`Kartu ucapan: ${o.pesanKartu.trim() || "(teks menyusul)"}`);
    if (o.catatan.trim()) opts.push(`Catatan: ${o.catatan.trim()}`);
    if (o.refName) opts.push(`Foto referensi: ${o.refName} (dikirim via chat)`);
    const key = m.name + "|" + opts.join("|");
    const qty = Math.max(1, parseInt(o.qty, 10) || 1);
    this.trackFunnel("add_to_cart");
    if (!this.state.cartOpen) this.trackFunnel("view_cart");
    this.setState(s => {
      const found = s.cart.find(i => i.key === key);
      const cart = found
        ? s.cart.map(i => (i.key === key ? { ...i, qty: i.qty + qty } : i))
        : [...s.cart, { key, name: m.name, kategori: m.kategori, stock: m.stock,
            isCustom: !!m.isCustom, priceLabel: m.priceLabel, price: m.price, qty, opts, hasRef: !!o.refName }];
      return { cart, cartOpen: true, cartStep: s.cartOpen ? s.cartStep : 1, modal: null, hint: "" };
    });
  };

  setBuyer(key, value) {
    this.setState(s => ({ buyer: { ...s.buyer, [key]: value }, hint: "" }));
  }

  setQty(key, delta) {
    if (delta > 0 && this.state.cart.some(i => i.key === key)) this.trackFunnel("add_to_cart");
    this.setState(s => ({
      cart: s.cart
        .map(i => (i.key === key ? { ...i, qty: i.qty + delta } : i))
        .filter(i => i.qty > 0)
    }));
  }

  handleAdd = (e) => {
    if (this.LIBUR) return this.setState({ hint: this.LIBUR_MSG });
    this.catatStat("keranjang", "-");
    if (this.state.payErr) this.setState({ payErr: "" });
    let root = e.currentTarget;
    while (root && !root.querySelector("h3")) root = root.parentElement;
    const name = (root?.querySelector("h3")?.textContent || "Boneka Crochet").trim();
    const priceEl = Array.from(root?.querySelectorAll("p") || []).find(p => /rp/i.test(p.textContent));
    const priceLabel = (priceEl?.textContent || "Harga menyusul").trim();
    const price = parseInt((priceLabel.match(/[\d.]+/) || ["0"])[0].replace(/\./g, ""), 10) || 0;
    this.trackFunnel("add_to_cart");
    if (!this.state.cartOpen) this.trackFunnel("view_cart");
    this.setState(s => {
      const found = s.cart.find(i => i.key === name);
      const cart = found
        ? s.cart.map(i => (i.key === name ? { ...i, qty: i.qty + 1 } : i))
        : [...s.cart, { key: name, name, priceLabel, price, qty: 1, opts: [] }];
      return { cart, cartOpen: true, cartStep: s.cartOpen ? s.cartStep : 1, hint: "" };
    });
  };

  waText(msg) {
    let raw = (this.props.whatsappNumber ?? "085281397730").replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
    if (raw.startsWith("0")) raw = "62" + raw.slice(1);
    return "https://wa.me/" + raw + "?text=" + encodeURIComponent(msg);
  }

  waProduct(it) {
    const rule = "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━";
    if (it.isCustom) {
      return this.waText(
        "*PESAN CUSTOM - CROCHET BY MACHEL*\n" + rule + "\n" +
        "Produk : " + it.name + "\n" +
        "Kategori : " + it.kategori + "\n" +
        "Harga : " + it.priceLabel + "\n" +
        "Status : Pre-order (bisa custom)\n" + rule + "\n\n" +
        "Halo Kak Machel, aku mau pesan produk ini dengan custom. Ini detailnya:\n\n" +
        "• Tema / karakter : \n" +
        "• Warna : \n" +
        "• Ukuran : \n" +
        "• Jumlah : \n" +
        "• Pakai kartu ucapan : ya / tidak\n" +
        "• Dibutuhkan tanggal : \n\n" +
        "Foto referensinya aku kirim di chat ini ya. Boleh dibantu estimasi harga dan lama pengerjaannya? Terima kasih!"
      );
    }
    return this.waText(
      "*TANYA PRODUK - CROCHET BY MACHEL*\n" + rule + "\n" +
      "Produk : " + it.name + "\n" +
      "Kategori : " + it.kategori + "\n" +
      "Harga : " + it.priceLabel + "\n" +
      "Status : " + it.stock + "\n" + rule + "\n\n" +
      "Halo Kak Machel, aku tertarik dengan produk ini. Boleh info ukuran, bahan, dan lama pengerjaannya? Terima kasih!"
    );
  }

  /** Produk pre-order: custom, stok habis, atau statusnya bukan "Ready". */
  itemPo(i) {
    if (!i) return false;
    if (i.isCustom) return true;
    return /pre|inden|habis|kosong|\bpo\b/i.test(String(i.stock || ""));
  }

  addItem(it, jml) {
    if (this.LIBUR) return this.setState({ hint: this.LIBUR_MSG });
    const key = it.name + "|" + it.kategori;
    const n = Math.max(1, parseInt(jml, 10) || 1);
    const existing = this.state.cart.find(i => i.key === key);
    if (!existing || existing.qty < (it.qty !== null && it.qty > 0 ? it.qty : 99)) this.trackFunnel("add_to_cart");
    if (!this.state.cartOpen) this.trackFunnel("view_cart");
    this.setState(s => {
      const found = s.cart.find(i => i.key === key);
      const max = it.qty !== null && it.qty > 0 ? it.qty : 99;
      const cart = found
        ? s.cart.map(i => (i.key === key ? { ...i, qty: Math.min(max, i.qty + n) } : i))
        : [...s.cart, {
            key,
            name: it.name,
            kategori: it.kategori,
            desc: it.desc,
            stock: it.stock,
            stockQty: it.qty,
            payLink: it.payLink,
            isCustom: it.isCustom,
            priceLabel: it.priceLabel,
            price: it.priceRaw || 0,
            photo: (it.images[0] && it.images[0].url) || "",
            qty: Math.min(max, n),
            opts: []
          }];
      return { cart, cartOpen: true, cartStep: s.cartOpen ? s.cartStep : 1, hint: "", detail: null };
    });
  }

  /**
   * Satu-satunya tempat yang mengatur kunci gulir dan alamat halaman.
   *
   * Sebelumnya setiap jalan keluar dari detail melepas kuncinya sendiri, dan
   * setiap jalan yang lupa meninggalkan halaman tidak bisa digulir. Sekarang
   * keadaan layar dihitung dari state, jadi tidak ada jalan yang bisa lupa.
   */
  syncOverlay() {
    const s = this.state;
    // cartOpen sengaja TIDAK ikut: keranjang adalah panel mengapung dengan
    // gulirnya sendiri, dan mengunci body membuat isi checkout yang lebih
    // tinggi dari panel jadi tidak bisa dijangkau sama sekali.
    const adaOverlay = !!(s.detail || s.lb.open || s.modal || (s.trk && s.trk.open));
    try {
      document.body.style.overflow = adaOverlay ? "hidden" : "";
    } catch (e) {}

    // ?produk= hanya sah selama detailnya terbuka. Begitu ditutup - lewat jalan
    // apa pun, termasuk masuk keranjang - alamatnya dibersihkan.
    try {
      if (!s.detail && /[?&]produk=/.test(location.search)) {
        history.replaceState(null, "", location.pathname + "#koleksi");
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  /* ---------- halaman detail produk ---------- */

  bukaDetail = (it) => {
    this.catatStat("lihat_produk", it.name);
    if (this.state.detail !== it.name + "|" + it.kategori) this.trackFunnel("view_item");
    this.setState({ detail: it.name + "|" + it.kategori, detailImg: 0, detailQty: 1 });
    try {
      history.replaceState(null, "", location.pathname + "?produk=" + encodeURIComponent(this.slugKe(it.name)) + "#koleksi");
    } catch (e) {}
  };

  tutupDetail = () => { this.setState({ detail: null }); };

  /** Geser jari pada galeri detail. Ambang 45px supaya ketukan tidak terbaca geseran. */
  detailSwipeMulai = e => {
    const t = e.touches && e.touches[0];
    this._swipe = t ? { x: t.clientX, y: t.clientY } : null;
  };

  detailSwipeSelesai = e => {
    const s = this._swipe;
    const t = e.changedTouches && e.changedTouches[0];
    this._swipe = null;
    if (!s || !t) return;
    const dx = t.clientX - s.x;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 45 || Math.abs(dx) < Math.abs(t.clientY - s.y)) return;
    this.setState(st => ({ detailImg: Math.max(0, (st.detailImg || 0) + (dx < 0 ? 1 : -1)) }));
  };

  /** Klik di mana pun pada kartu membuka detail, kecuali pada tombol atau tautan. */
  kartuKlik = it => e => {
    if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest("button,a")) return;
    this.bukaDetail(it);
  };

  /**
   * Menyuntikkan data terstruktur Product untuk setiap produk di katalog.
   *
   * Katalog datang dari spreadsheet saat halaman berjalan, jadi tidak bisa
   * ditulis di <head> sebagai teks statis. Google merender JavaScript sebelum
   * mengindeks, jadi blok yang disuntikkan begini tetap terbaca - dan inilah
   * yang memunculkan harga, stok, serta bintang ulasan di hasil pencarian.
   */
  /** Katalog mentah dari spreadsheet, sudah dinormalkan. Dipakai data terstruktur. */
  daftarKatalog() {
    const rows = this.state.rows;
    if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return [];
    return rows.map(r => this.normalize(r));
  }

  suntikProdukLD(items) {
    if (this._ldDone || !items || !items.length) return;
    this._ldDone = true;

    const dasar = "https://" + String(this.props.siteDomain || "machelcrochet.my.id").trim().replace(/^https?:\/\//, "").replace(/\/.*$/, "");
    const ulasanSemua = this.state.reviews || [];

    const daftar = items.slice(0, 40).map((it, n) => {
      const slug = this.slugKe(it.name);
      const nm = it.name.trim().toLowerCase();
      const ulasan = ulasanSemua
        .filter(r => String(r.produk || r.product || "").trim().toLowerCase() === nm)
        .map(r => {
          const rating = Number(String(r.rating || r.bintang || "").replace(",", "."));
          const review = {
            "@type": "Review",
            reviewRating: { "@type": "Rating", ratingValue: rating, bestRating: 5, worstRating: 1 },
            author: { "@type": "Person", name: r.nama },
            reviewBody: r.ulasan || r.review
          };
          const tanggal = String(r.waktu || r.tanggal || "").trim().split(" ")[0].match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
          if (tanggal) {
            const iso = tanggal[3] + "-" + tanggal[2] + "-" + tanggal[1];
            const date = new Date(iso + "T00:00:00Z");
            if (Number.isFinite(date.getTime()) && date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === iso) review.datePublished = iso;
          }
          return review;
        })
        .filter(r => r.reviewRating.ratingValue >= 1 && r.reviewRating.ratingValue <= 5
          && typeof r.author.name === "string" && r.author.name.trim()
          && typeof r.reviewBody === "string" && r.reviewBody.trim());
      const bintang = ulasan.map(r => r.reviewRating.ratingValue);

      const produk = {
        "@type": "Product",
        "@id": dasar + "/?produk=" + encodeURIComponent(slug) + "#produk",
        name: it.name,
        description: (it.desc || (it.name + " - boneka rajut handmade.")).slice(0, 400),
        category: it.kategori || "Boneka Rajut",
        brand: { "@type": "Brand", name: "Machel Crochet" },
        url: dasar + "/?produk=" + encodeURIComponent(slug) + "#koleksi"
      };
      // images punya dua bentuk: keluaran normalize() berisi array kandidat URL,
      // sedangkan item katalog siap-render berisi objek {url, alts}. Google
      // MEWAJIBKAN image pada Product - tanpa itu seluruh cuplikan harga dan
      // bintang tidak muncul, jadi keduanya diterima di sini.
      const kandidat = it.images && it.images[0];
      const foto = Array.isArray(kandidat) ? kandidat[0] : (kandidat && kandidat.url);
      if (foto && /^https?:\/\//.test(foto)) produk.image = foto;

      if (it.priceRaw > 0) {
        produk.offers = {
          "@type": "Offer",
          price: it.priceRaw,
          priceCurrency: "IDR",
          availability: it.qty > 0
            ? "https://schema.org/InStock"
            : "https://schema.org/PreOrder",
          itemCondition: "https://schema.org/NewCondition",
          url: produk.url,
          seller: { "@type": "Organization", name: "Machel Crochet" }
        };
      }
      if (bintang.length) {
        produk.review = ulasan.sort((a, b) => (b.datePublished || "").localeCompare(a.datePublished || "")).slice(0, 5);
        produk.aggregateRating = {
          "@type": "AggregateRating",
          ratingValue: (bintang.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / bintang.length).toFixed(1),
          reviewCount: bintang.length,
          bestRating: 5, worstRating: 1
        };
      }
      return { "@type": "ListItem", position: n + 1, item: produk };
    });

    try {
      const s = document.createElement("script");
      s.type = "application/ld+json";
      s.textContent = JSON.stringify({
        "@context": "https://schema.org",
        "@type": "ItemList",
        name: "Koleksi boneka rajut Machel Crochet",
        numberOfItems: daftar.length,
        itemListElement: daftar
      });
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  /** Nilai untuk panel detail. Dipisah dari renderVals supaya tetap terbaca. */
  detailVals(items) {
    const kunci = this.state.detail;
    const it = kunci ? items.find(x => x.name + "|" + x.kategori === kunci) : null;
    if (!it) return { detailOpen: false, detailFotos: [], detailPoin: [] };

    const maks = it.qty !== null && it.qty > 0 ? it.qty : 99;
    const jml = Math.min(maks, Math.max(1, this.state.detailQty || 1));
    const i = Math.min(Math.max(0, this.state.detailImg || 0), it.images.length - 1);

    // Ulasan produk ini saja. Nama produk di tab Review tidak selalu ditulis
    // persis sama, jadi kecocokan longgar: salah satu memuat yang lain.
    const nm = it.name.toLowerCase();
    const ulasan = (this.state.reviews || []).filter(r => {
      const p = String(r.produk || r.product || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!p) return false;
      return p === nm || p.includes(nm) || nm.includes(p);
    });
    const bintang = ulasan
      .map(r => parseFloat(String(r.rating || r.bintang || "").replace(",", ".")))
      .filter(n => n >= 1 && n <= 5);
    const rata = bintang.length
      ? (bintang.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / bintang.length) : 0;

    const bulat = n => "★★★★★".slice(0, n) + "☆☆☆☆☆".slice(0, 5 - n);
    const kartuUlasan = ulasan
      .filter(r => String(r.ulasan || r.review || "").trim())
      .slice(0, 4)
      .map(r => {
        const n = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, parseInt(String(r.rating || r.bintang || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""), 10) || 5));
        return {
          teks: String(r.ulasan || r.review || "").trim(),
          oleh: String(r.nama || r.name || "Pelanggan").trim(),
          bintang: bulat(n),
          tgl: String(r.waktu || r.tanggal || "").trim().split(" ")[0],
          terverifikasi: /^cbm-/i.test(String(r.order_id || r.orderid || "").trim())
        };
      });

    const poin = [
      { ikon: "kirim", judul: it.stock === "Ready" ? "Siap kirim" : "Dirajut setelah pesanan masuk",
        isi: it.stock === "Ready" ? "Dikemas hari yang sama untuk pesanan sebelum jam 3 siang." : "Pengerjaan 3-10 hari, tergantung tingkat kerumitan." },
      { ikon: "tukar", judul: "7 hari pengembalian", isi: "Rusak, cacat rajutan, atau salah kirim - kami ganti atau kembalikan dana." },
      { ikon: "tangan", judul: "Dirajut satu per satu", isi: "Tidak ada dua boneka yang benar-benar identik, dan itu memang maksudnya." }
    ];

    return {
      detailOpen: true,
      detailNama: it.name,
      detailKategori: it.kategori,
      detailDesc: it.desc || "Boneka rajut buatan tangan. Tanya lewat WhatsApp kalau butuh detail tambahan.",
      detailHarga: it.priceLabel,
      detailStokLabel: it.stockLabel,
      detailStokBg: it.stockBg,
      detailStokFg: it.stockColor,
      detailStokNote: it.stockNote,
      detailBanyakFoto: it.images.length > 1,
      // Geseran halus: satu jalur berisi semua foto, digeser pakai transform.
      // Nilai persennya hidup saat runtime, jadi memang harus lewat hole.
      detailTrackGeser: "translate3d(" + (-i * 100) + "%,0,0)",
      detailSlides: it.images.map((im, n) => ({
        url: im.url, alts: im.alts, alt: it.name + " foto " + (n + 1),
        zoom: () => this.openLightbox(
          it.images.map((x, k) => ({ url: x.url, alts: x.alts, alt: it.name + " foto " + (k + 1) })), n, it.name)
      })),
      detailFotos: it.images.map((im, n) => ({
        url: im.url, alts: im.alts, alt: it.name + " foto kecil " + (n + 1),
        br: n === i ? "#55763F" : "#E4EBD5",
        opasitas: n === i ? "1" : ".62",
        pilih: () => this.setState({ detailImg: n })
      })),
      detailTitik: it.images.map((im, n) => ({
        bg: n === i ? "#55763F" : "rgba(85,118,63,.28)",
        lebar: n === i ? "18px" : "6px",
        pilih: () => this.setState({ detailImg: n })
      })),
      detailSwipeMulai: this.detailSwipeMulai,
      detailSwipeSelesai: this.detailSwipeSelesai,
      detailUlasan: kartuUlasan,
      detailAdaUlasan: kartuUlasan.length > 0,
      detailUlasanJudul: kartuUlasan.length === 1 ? "1 ulasan untuk produk ini" : kartuUlasan.length + " ulasan untuk produk ini",
      detailFotoPrev: () => this.setState(s => ({ detailImg: Math.max(0, (s.detailImg || 0) - 1) })),
      detailFotoNext: () => this.setState(s => ({ detailImg: Math.min(it.images.length - 1, (s.detailImg || 0) + 1) })),
      detailHitung: i + 1 + " / " + it.images.length,
      detailAdaRating: bintang.length > 0,
      detailRata: rata ? rata.toFixed(1) : "",
      detailBintang: rata ? "★★★★★".slice(0, Math.round(rata)) + "☆☆☆☆☆".slice(0, 5 - Math.round(rata)) : "",
      detailJmlUlasan: bintang.length + " ulasan",
      detailQty: jml,
      detailQtyKurang: () => this.setState(s => ({ detailQty: Math.max(1, (s.detailQty || 1) - 1) })),
      detailQtyTambah: () => this.setState(s => ({ detailQty: Math.min(maks, (s.detailQty || 1) + 1) })),
      detailQtyKurangWarna: jml <= 1 ? "#C3CDB4" : "#55763F",
      detailQtyTambahWarna: jml >= maks ? "#C3CDB4" : "#55763F",
      detailTotal: it.priceRaw ? this.displayMoney(it.priceRaw * jml) : it.priceLabel,
      detailAdaTotal: !!it.priceRaw && jml > 1,
      detailBisaBeli: !it.isCustom,
      detailCustom: it.isCustom,
      detailBeliLabel: this.LIBUR ? "Toko Libur" : (it.stock === "Ready" ? "Masukkan Keranjang" : "Pesan Pre-order"),
      detailBeliBg: this.LIBUR ? "#B7C3AA" : (it.stock === "Ready" ? "#2F7A52" : "#B4802F"),
      detailWa: it.waChat,
      detailTambah: () => this.addItem(it, jml),
      detailPoin: poin.map(p => ({ ...p, ikonKirim: p.ikon === "kirim", ikonTukar: p.ikon === "tukar", ikonTangan: p.ikon === "tangan" })),
      tutupDetail: this.tutupDetail
    };
  }

  jalankanDiagnosa() {
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    const tulis = t => this.setState(s => ({ diag: (s.diag || "") + t + "\n" }));
    this.setState({ diag: "" });

    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return tulis("✕ Order Api Url kosong / bukan http. Isi di Tweaks.");
    tulis("URL konektor:\n" + api + "\n");

    // 1. cek GET (izin akses & versi konektor)
    fetch(api + (api.indexOf("?") > -1 ? "&" : "?") + "action=ongkir&berat=250&belanja=0")
      .then(r => r.text().then(t => ({ status: r.status, t, url: r.url })))
      .then(({ status, t, url }) => {
        if (/accounts\.google\.com|signin/i.test(url) || /<html/i.test(t.slice(0, 200))) {
          tulis("✕ Konektor meminta LOGIN GOOGLE.");
          tulis("  Perbaiki: Deploy → Manage deployments → ✏ →");
          tulis("  Who has access = ANYONE (bukan 'Anyone with Google account')");
          tulis("  Execute as = ME → Deploy.");
          return;
        }
        let j = null;
        try { j = JSON.parse(t); } catch (e) {}
        if (!j) return tulis("✕ Jawaban bukan JSON (status " + status + "):\n" + t.slice(0, 200));
        if (j.layanan) tulis("✓ GET jalan - konektor versi BARU (ongkir dikenali).");
        else tulis("⚠ GET jalan, tapi 'action=ongkir' belum dikenali → konektor masih versi LAMA. Deploy New version.");

        // 2. cek POST invoice
        tulis("\nMenguji pembuatan tagihan…");
        return fetch(api, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            type: "invoice", orderId: "DIAG-" + Date.now(), total: 10000, channel: "Diagnosa",
            buyer: { name: "Tes Diagnosa", phone: "081200000000", email: "tes@contoh.com" },
            items: [{ name: "Tes produk", kategori: "Tes", qty: 1, price: 10000, status: "Ready", isCustom: false, opts: "" }]
          })
        }).then(r => r.text()).then(t2 => {
          let j2 = null;
          try { j2 = JSON.parse(t2); } catch (e) {}
          if (j2 && j2.link) tulis("✓ TAGIHAN BERHASIL DIBUAT.\n  " + j2.link + "\n\nPembayaran otomatis siap dipakai.");
          else if (j2) tulis("✕ Konektor menolak:\n  " + (j2.message || t2.slice(0, 200)) + (j2.detail ? "\n  " + j2.detail : ""));
          else tulis("✕ Jawaban POST bukan JSON:\n" + t2.slice(0, 200));
        });
      })
      .catch(e => {
        tulis("✕ Browser tidak bisa menghubungi konektor (" + (e.message || e) + ").");
        tulis("  Hampir selalu karena Who has access ≠ Anyone.");
      });
  }

  setShip(patch) { this.setState(s => ({ ship: { ...s.ship, ...patch } })); }

  onShipQ = e => {
    const v = e.target.value;
    this.setShip({ q: v, err: "" });
    clearTimeout(this.shipTimer);
    if (v.trim().length < 3) return this.setShip({ hasil: [], cari: false });
    this.setShip({ cari: true });
    this.shipTimer = setTimeout(() => this.cariKota(v.trim()), 450);
  };

  cariKota(q) {
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return this.setShip({ cari: false });
    ambilUlang(api + (api.indexOf("?") > -1 ? "&" : "?") + "action=kota&q=" + encodeURIComponent(q), 2)
      .then(res => {
        const hasil = (res && res.hasil) || [];
        if (hasil.length) return this.setShip({ cari: false, hasil: hasil, flat: false });
        // tujuan tak dikenal RajaOngkir -> pakai tarif cadangan dari Pengaturan
        this.setShip({ cari: false, hasil: [], flat: true });
        if (!this.state.ship.layanan.length) this.cekOngkir(null);
      })
      .catch(() => this.setShip({ cari: false, hasil: [], flat: true }));
  }

  pilihKota(k) {
    this.setShip({ tujuan: k, q: k.label, hasil: [], layanan: [], pilih: null });
    setTimeout(() => this.cekOngkir(k), 60);
  }

  cekOngkir(tujuanArg) {
    const tujuan = tujuanArg || this.state.ship.tujuan;
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    const cart = this.state.cart;
    if (!cart.length) return this.setShip({ err: "Keranjang masih kosong." });
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return this.setShip({ flat: true, err: "Cek ongkir otomatis belum aktif. Ongkir kami konfirmasi lewat WhatsApp sebelum pembayaran." });

    const berat = cart.reduce((t, i) => t + (Number(i.berat) || Number(this.props.itemWeightGram) || 250) * i.qty, 0);
    const belanja = cart.reduce((t, i) => t + i.price * i.qty, 0);

    this.setShip({ busy: true, err: "", layanan: [], pilih: null });
    ambilUlang(api + (api.indexOf("?") > -1 ? "&" : "?") + "action=ongkir&berat=" + berat +
      "&belanja=" + belanja + "&tujuan=" + encodeURIComponent(tujuan ? tujuan.id : ""), 2)
      .then(res => {
        const list = (res && res.layanan) || [];
        if (!list.length) return this.setShip({ busy: false, flat: true, err: "Belum ada layanan kurir untuk tujuan itu. Ongkir kami konfirmasi lewat WhatsApp sebelum pembayaran." });
        this.setShip({ busy: false, layanan: list, pilih: list[0], gratis: !!res.gratis });
      })
      .catch(() => this.setShip({ busy: false, flat: true, err: "Cek ongkir sedang bermasalah. Ongkir kami konfirmasi lewat WhatsApp sebelum pembayaran." }));
  }

  ongkirBiaya() {
    const p = this.state.ship.pilih;
    return p ? Number(p.biaya) || 0 : 0;
  }

  shippingPayload() {
    const s = this.state.ship;
    if (!s.pilih && !s.alamat.trim()) return null;
    return {
      tujuan: s.tujuan ? s.tujuan.label : "",
      alamat: (s.alamat.trim() + (s.tujuan ? "\n" + s.tujuan.label + (s.tujuan.pos ? " " + s.tujuan.pos : "") : "")).trim(),
      kurir: s.pilih ? s.pilih.kurir : "",
      layanan: s.pilih ? s.pilih.layanan : "",
      biaya: this.ongkirBiaya(),
      etd: s.pilih ? s.pilih.etd : ""
    };
  }

  buatOrderId() {
    let r = "";
    try {
      const b = new Uint8Array(3);
      crypto.getRandomValues(b);
      r = Array.from(b).map(x => "0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ"[x % 33]).join("");
    } catch (err) {
      r = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5).toUpperCase();
    }
    return "CBM-" + Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase() + "-" + r;
  }

  handleCheckout = (existingOrderId) => {
    if (this.LIBUR) return this.setState({ hint: this.LIBUR_MSG });
    this.catatStat("checkout", "-");
    const { cart, buyer } = this.state;
    if (!cart.length) return this.setState({ hint: "Keranjang masih kosong." });
    const salah = this.cekBiodata(buyer);
    if (Object.keys(salah).length) return this.tandaiBiodata(salah);
    this.setState({ bioErr: null });
    if (!(typeof existingOrderId === "string" && existingOrderId)) this.trackFunnel("select_payment_method", "whatsapp");
    const rule = "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━";
    const lines = cart.map((i, n) => {
      const sub = i.price ? "Rp" + (i.price * i.qty).toLocaleString("id-ID") : "-";
      return [
        `${n + 1}. *${i.name}*`,
        "    Kategori : " + (i.kategori || "-"),
        "    Status : " + (this.itemPo(i) ? "Pre-order (dirajut setelah pesanan)" : (i.stock || "-")),
        "    Harga : " + i.priceLabel,
        "    Jumlah : " + i.qty + " pcs",
        "    Subtotal : " + sub,
        i.desc ? "    Keterangan : " + i.desc : "",
        i.photo ? "    Foto : " + i.photo : "",
        (i.opts && i.opts.length) ? "    " + i.opts.join("\n    ") : ""
      ].filter(Boolean).join("\n");
    });
    const needRef = cart.some(i => i.hasRef);
    const kirim = this.shippingPayload();
    const subtotal = cart.reduce((t, i) => t + i.price * i.qty, 0);
    const total = subtotal + (kirim ? kirim.biaya : 0);
    const text =
      `*PESANAN BARU - CROCHET BY MACHEL*\n${rule}\n\n` +
      `*DETAIL PESANAN*\n${lines.join("\n\n")}\n\n` +
      (subtotal ? `Subtotal produk : Rp${subtotal.toLocaleString("id-ID")}\n` : "") +
      (kirim && kirim.biaya ? `Ongkos kirim : Rp${kirim.biaya.toLocaleString("id-ID")}\n` : "") +
      (total ? `*Estimasi total: Rp${total.toLocaleString("id-ID")}*\n\n` : "") +
      `${rule}\n*DATA PEMESAN*\nNama : ${buyer.name.trim()}\nNo. HP : ${buyer.phone.trim()}\nEmail : ${buyer.email.trim()}\n` +
      (kirim ? `\n*PENGIRIMAN*\nAlamat : ${kirim.alamat.replace(/\n/g, ", ")}\n` +
        (kirim.kurir ? `Kurir : ${kirim.kurir} ${kirim.layanan} (${kirim.etd})\nOngkir : Rp${kirim.biaya.toLocaleString("id-ID")}\n` : "") : "") +
      `${rule}\n\n` +
      "Halo Kak Machel, aku mau lanjut pesanan di atas. Boleh dibantu konfirmasi ketersediaan, detail custom, dan pengirimannya? Terima kasih!" +
      (needRef ? "\n\n(Foto referensinya aku kirim menyusul di chat ini ya.)" : "");
    const orderId = typeof existingOrderId === "string" && existingOrderId ? existingOrderId : this.buatOrderId();
    if (orderId !== existingOrderId) this.recordOrder(orderId, total, "WhatsApp");
    const done = () => this.setState({ hint: "No. pesanan " + orderId + " · chat WhatsApp terbuka dengan detailnya." });
    if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, done);
    else done();
    this.trackWhatsapp();
    window.open(this.waText("No. Pesanan : " + orderId + "\n" + text), "_blank");
  };

  handlePay = () => {
    if (this.LIBUR) return this.setState({ hint: this.LIBUR_MSG });
    const { cart, buyer } = this.state;
    if (!cart.length) return this.setState({ hint: "Keranjang masih kosong." });
    const salah = this.cekBiodata(buyer);
    if (Object.keys(salah).length) return this.tandaiBiodata(salah);
    this.setState({ bioErr: null, payErr: "" });   // percobaan baru: pesan lama dibuang
    if (this.state.paying) return;
    this.trackFunnel("select_payment_method", "payment_link");

    const total = cart.reduce((t, i) => t + i.price * i.qty, 0) + this.ongkirBiaya();
    const orderId = this.buatOrderId();
    const api = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    const single = cart.length === 1 && cart[0].payLink ? cart[0] : null;

    // 1 produk & sudah punya link sendiri -> langsung buka (paling cepat)
    if (single) {
      const url1 = this.payUrl(single.payLink, orderId, single);
      this.recordOrder(orderId, total, "Payment link", url1);
      this.setState({ hint: "No. pesanan " + orderId + " · halaman pembayaran terbuka." });
      this.trackPaymentLink();
      window.open(url1, "_blank");
      return;
    }

    // beberapa produk -> minta satu invoice gabungan ke konektor
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(api)) return this.payFallback(orderId, total);

    this.setState({ paying: true, hint: "Membuat tagihan untuk " + cart.length + " produk…" });
    const timeout = new Promise((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(new Error("timeout")), 45000));
    Promise.race([
      fetch(api, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...this.orderPayload(orderId, total, "Invoice gabungan"), type: "invoice" })
      }).then(r => r.json()),
      timeout
    ])
      .then(res => {
        const link = res && res.ok && (res.link || (res.data && res.data.link));
        if (!link) {
          const err = new Error("no link");
          err.sebab = (res && (res.message || res.detail)) || "konektor tidak mengembalikan link";
          throw err;
        }
        // An invoice is unpaid; never emit Purchase here.
        this.setState({ paying: false, hint: "No. pesanan " + orderId + " · membuka halaman pembayaran…" });
        this.trackPaymentLink();
        window.location.href = link;
      })
      .catch(e => {
        const sebab = e && e.sebab ? e.sebab : (e && e.message === "timeout"
          ? "konektor tidak merespons (cek Deploy New version di Apps Script)"
          : "koneksi ke konektor gagal");
        console.warn("[Crochet] Tagihan gagal dibuat:", sebab);
        this.setState({ paying: false });
        this.payFallback(orderId, total, sebab);
      });
  };

  payFallback(orderId, total, sebab) {
    const cart = this.state.cart;
    const links = cart.filter(i => i.payLink);
    if (links.length === 1) {
      const u = this.payUrl(links[0].payLink, orderId, links[0]);
      this.recordOrder(orderId, total, "Payment link", u);
      this.setState({ hint: "No. pesanan " + orderId + " · halaman pembayaran terbuka." });
      this.trackPaymentLink();
      return window.open(u, "_blank");
    }
    // Pencatatan diserahkan ke handleCheckout supaya nomor pesanannya satu saja.
    // Alasannya disimpan terpisah agar tidak tertimpa pesan "chat terbuka".
    this.setState({ payErr: sebab || "konektor tidak merespons" });
    this.handleCheckout(orderId);
  }

  payUrl(link, orderId, item) {
    const b = this.state.buyer;
    const name = b.name.trim();
    const email = b.email.trim();
    // Mayar menolak format "62xxx" tanpa plus - pakai format lokal 08xxx
    let digits = b.phone.replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
    if (digits.startsWith("62")) digits = "0" + digits.slice(2);
    else if (!digits.startsWith("0")) digits = "0" + digits;
    const phone = digits;
    const phoneIntl = "+62" + digits.replace(/^0/, "");
    // nama parameter berbeda tiap penyedia (Mayar / Tripay / Midtrans) -
    // dikirim semua aliasnya, yang tidak dikenali diabaikan.
    const params = {
      name: name, customerName: name, customer_name: name, nama: name,
      email: email, customerEmail: email, customer_email: email,
      mobile: phone, phone: phone, customerMobile: phone, customer_phone: phone, no_hp: phone,
      whatsapp: phone, phoneNumber: phoneIntl,
      qty: item.qty,
      ref: orderId, reference: orderId, merchantRef: orderId, merchant_ref: orderId, order_id: orderId
    };
    try {
      const u = new URL(link);
      Object.keys(params).forEach(k => {
        if (params[k] !== "" && params[k] != null && !u.searchParams.has(k)) {
          u.searchParams.set(k, params[k]);
        }
      });
      return u.toString();
    } catch (err) {
      return link;
    }
  }

  orderPayload(orderId, total, channel) {
    const { cart, buyer } = this.state;
    let hp = buyer.phone.replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
    if (hp.startsWith("62")) hp = "0" + hp.slice(2);
    else if (!hp.startsWith("0")) hp = "0" + hp;
    return {
      orderId,
      channel,
      total,
      buyer: { name: buyer.name.trim(), phone: hp, email: buyer.email.trim() },
      
      adAttribution: ['gclid', 'gbraid', 'wbraid'].reduce((acc, k) => {
        try { const v = localStorage.getItem('cbm_' + k); if (v) acc[k] = v; } catch(e) {}
        return acc;
      }, {}),
      shipping: this.shippingPayload(),
      items: cart.map(i => ({
        name: i.name,
        kategori: i.kategori,
        qty: i.qty,
        price: i.price,
        status: this.itemPo(i) ? "Pre-order" : (i.stock || "Ready"),
        isCustom: !!i.isCustom,
        preorder: this.itemPo(i),
        opts: (i.opts || []).join(" | ")
      }))
    };
  }

  recordOrder(orderId, total, channel, linkBayar) {
    const url = String(this.props.orderApiUrl || "").trim();
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) return;
    try {
      fetch(url, {
        method: "POST",
        mode: "no-cors",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...this.orderPayload(orderId, total, channel), type: "order", linkBayar: linkBayar || "" })
      });
    } catch (err) { /* pesanan tetap lanjut via WhatsApp */ }
  }

  /**
   * Memuat Google tag (gtag.js) sekali saja, kalau ID-nya diisi.
   * Dipakai Google Ads untuk pengukuran konversi dan Merchant Center untuk
   * menautkan iklan ke pesanan yang benar-benar terjadi.
   */
  /**
   * Memuat container Google Tag Manager sekali saja.
   *
   * Berdampingan dengan gtag.js: keduanya menulis ke dataLayer yang sama, jadi
   * konversi tetap terkirim lewat jalur langsung meskipun tag di GTM belum
   * dikonfigurasi. Cuplikan <noscript> dari Google sengaja tidak dipasang -
   * halaman ini dirender JavaScript, jadi pengunjung tanpa JS tidak melihat
   * apa pun untuk diukur.
   */
  muatGTM() {
    const id = String(this.props.gtmId || "").trim();
    if (!id || this._gtmDimuat) return;
    this._gtmDimuat = true;

    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
    window.dataLayer.push({ "gtm.start": Date.now(), event: "gtm.js" });

    const s = document.createElement("script");
    s.async = true;
    s.src = "https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=" + encodeURIComponent(id);
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }

  muatGoogleTag() {
    const id = String(this.props.googleTagId || "").trim();
    if (!id || this._gtagDimuat) return;
    this._gtagDimuat = true;

    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
    window.gtag = window.gtag || function () { window.dataLayer.push(arguments); };
    window.gtag("js", new Date());
    window.gtag("config", id);

    const s = document.createElement("script");
    s.async = true;
    s.src = "https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=" + encodeURIComponent(id);
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }

  /**
   * Melaporkan satu konversi pesanan ke Google Ads.
   *
   * transaction_id memakai nomor pesanan, supaya satu pesanan tidak terhitung
   * dua kali kalau pembeli memuat ulang halaman atau mengulang checkout.
   */
  /**
   * Konversi "Memulai checkout" - dikirim saat pembeli membuka langkah data
   * pengiriman, bukan saat pesanan jadi.
   *
   * Sengaja TANPA transaction_id: nomor pesanan belum ada pada tahap ini, dan
   * Google Ads menghitung tindakan ini per sesi, bukan per transaksi.
   */
  lacakCheckout() {
    this.trackFunnel("begin_checkout");
    const id = String(this.props.googleTagId || "").trim();
    const label = String(this.props.googleAdsCheckoutLabel || "").trim();
    if (typeof window.gtag !== "function") return;

    // Item keranjang menyimpan harganya sebagai `price` (priceRaw hanya ada
    // pada item katalog), jadi field inilah yang dipakai semua perhitungan
    // total lain di file ini.
    const nilai = this.state.cart.reduce((t, i) => t + (i.price || 0) * i.qty, 0);
    try {
      if (id && label && /^AW-/i.test(id)) {
        window.gtag("event", "conversion", { send_to: id + "/" + label, value: nilai, currency: "IDR" });
      }
      window.gtag("event", "begin_checkout", { value: nilai, currency: "IDR" });

    } catch (err) {}
  }

  lacakKonversi(orderId, total, channel) {
    const id = String(this.props.googleTagId || "").trim();
    if (!id || typeof window.gtag !== "function") return;

    const label = String(this.props.googleAdsConversionLabel || "").trim();
    const nilai = Number(total) || 0;

    try {
      // Juga didorong ke dataLayer supaya tag mana pun yang kamu buat di GTM
      // bisa memakai pesanan ini sebagai pemicu.
      window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
      window.dataLayer.push({
        event: "purchase", transaction_id: orderId, value: nilai, currency: "IDR"
      });

      if (label && /^AW-/i.test(id)) {
        window.gtag("event", "conversion", {
          send_to: id + "/" + label,
          value: nilai,
          currency: "IDR",
          transaction_id: orderId
        });
      }
      window.gtag("event", "purchase", {
        transaction_id: orderId,
        value: nilai,
        currency: "IDR",
        affiliation: channel || ""
      });
    } catch (err) { /* pengukuran gagal tidak boleh mengganggu pesanan */ }
  }

  renderVals() {
    this.syncBack();
    this.tick();
    let raw = (this.props.whatsappNumber ?? "085281397730").replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
    if (raw.startsWith("0")) raw = "62" + raw.slice(1);
    const rule0 = "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━";
    const waLink = this.waText(
      "*KONSULTASI - CROCHET BY MACHEL*\n" + rule0 + "\n\n" +
      "Halo Kak Machel, aku dari website Machel Crochet. Aku mau tanya-tanya dulu sebelum pesan:\n\n" +
      "• Boneka yang aku cari : \n" +
      "• Untuk keperluan : (hadiah / koleksi / lainnya)\n" +
      "• Perkiraan budget : \n" +
      "• Dibutuhkan tanggal : \n\n" +
      "Boleh dibantu rekomendasi dan estimasi harganya? Terima kasih!"
    );
    const waRequestLink = this.waText(
      "*REQUEST BONEKA CUSTOM - CROCHET BY MACHEL*\n" + rule0 + "\n\n" +
      "Halo Kak Machel, aku mau request boneka rajut custom. Ini gambaran idenya:\n\n" +
      "• Bentuk / karakter : \n" +
      "• Warna : \n" +
      "• Ukuran : \n" +
      "• Jumlah : \n" +
      "• Dibutuhkan tanggal : \n" +
      "• Perkiraan budget : \n\n" +
      "Foto referensinya aku kirim di chat ini ya. Boleh dibantu estimasi harga dan lama pengerjaannya? Terima kasih!"
    );
    this.waHref = waLink;
    const cart = this.state.cart;
    const total = cart.reduce((t, i) => t + i.price * i.qty, 0);
    const rows = (this.state.rows && this.state.rows.length) ? this.state.rows : [];
    const punyaUrutan = rows.some(r => String(r.urutan || "").trim() !== "");
    const rowsUrut = punyaUrutan
      ? rows.slice().sort((a, b) => {
          const na = parseInt(a.urutan, 10), nb = parseInt(b.urutan, 10);
          return (isNaN(na) ? 9999 : na) - (isNaN(nb) ? 9999 : nb);
        })
      : rows;
    const sheetItems = rowsUrut.map(r => this.normalize(r)).map(base => {
      // Stok nol BUKAN jalan buntu: produk tetap bisa dipesan sebagai pre-order.
      // Bedanya dengan produk custom - yang ini tombolnya "Beli", bukan "Chat & Pesan".
      const habis = base.stock === "Habis";
      const stock = (base.isCustom || habis) ? "Pre-order" : base.stock;

      liburBeliReady: this.LIBUR ? "Toko Libur" : "Beli Sekarang",
      liburBeliPo: this.LIBUR ? "Toko Libur" : "Beli Pre-order",
      liburTambah: this.LIBUR ? "Toko Libur \u2014 Checkout Ditutup" : "Tambah ke Keranjang",
      liburLanjut: this.LIBUR ? "Toko Libur" : "Lanjutkan",
      liburCheckout: this.LIBUR ? "Toko Libur \u2014 Checkout Ditutup" : "Checkout via WhatsApp",
      preAnim: this.state.leaving ? "preFade .5s ease forwards" : "none",
      trackWhatsapp: this.trackWhatsapp,
      trackPaymentLink: this.trackPaymentLink,
      waLink,
      waRequestLink,
      ...this.detailVals(sheetItems),
      sheetItems: pageItems,
      sheetEmpty: shown.length === 0,
      hasPages: totalPages > 1,
      pages,
      pageLabel: "Halaman " + page + " dari " + totalPages,

        // dipakai kartu produk, galeri, dan sosmed - coba kandidat berikutnya dulu
        const img = e.currentTarget;
        const rest = (img.dataset.alts || "").split(" ").filter(Boolean);
        if (rest.length) {
          img.dataset.alts = rest.slice(1).join(" ");
          img.src = rest[0];
        } else {
          img.onerror = null;
          img.removeAttribute("src");
          img.style.display = "none";
          const box = img.parentElement;
          if (box && !box.dataset.failed) {
            box.dataset.failed = "1";
            box.style.display = "flex";
            box.style.alignItems = "center";
            box.style.justifyContent = "center";
            box.style.padding = "18px";
            box.style.textAlign = "center";
            box.style.font = "500 12.5px/1.5 'DM Sans',system-ui,sans-serif";
            box.style.color = "#8E9A82";
            box.textContent = "Foto tidak bisa dimuat - pastikan file Drive di-share \u201cAnyone with the link\u201d";
          }
        }
      },
      slidePrev: e => this.slide(e, -1),
      slideNext: e => this.slide(e, 1),
      sheetNote:
        this.state.sheetStatus === "ok" ? "Stok & harga live dari Google Sheets."
        : this.state.sheetStatus === "loading" ? "Memuat data produk…"
        : this.state.sheetStatus === "error" ? "Gagal memuat Sheets - menampilkan data cadangan."

        dec: () => this.setQty(i.key, -1)

      cartEmpty: cart.length === 0,
      poAda: cart.some(i => this.itemPo(i)),
      poNote: (() => {
        const po = cart.filter(i => this.itemPo(i));
        if (!po.length) return "";
        return "Pre-order: " + po.map(i => i.name).join(", ") +
          " - dirajut satu per satu setelah pembayaran masuk, bukan stok siap kirim. " +
          "Jadwal pengerjaannya kami kabari lewat WhatsApp.";
      })(),
      cartCount: cart.reduce((t, i) => t + i.qty, 0),
      cartTotal: total ? this.displayMoney(total) : "Menyusul",
      cartOpen: this.state.cartOpen,
      toggleCart: () => {
        if (!this.state.cartOpen) this.trackFunnel("view_cart");
        this.setState(s => ({ cartOpen: !s.cartOpen, cartStep: s.cartOpen ? s.cartStep : 1 }));
      },
      dot2: this.state.cartStep === 2 ? "#7E9B62" : "#DBE4C9",
      cartStep1: this.state.cartStep !== 2,
      cartStep2: this.state.cartStep === 2,
      cartTitle: this.state.cartStep === 2 ? "Pengiriman & Data" : "Keranjang Kamu",
      cartRingkas: this.state.cart.reduce((t, i) => t + i.qty, 0) + " produk · " +

          ? this.displayMoney(this.state.cart.reduce((t, i) => t + i.price * i.qty, 0)) : "Menyusul"),

        if (this.LIBUR) return this.setState({ hint: this.LIBUR_MSG });

        const entering = this.state.cartStep !== 2;
        this.setState({ cartStep: 2, hint: "" });
        if (entering) this.lacakCheckout();
      },
      addToCart: this.handleAdd,
      buyerName: this.state.buyer.name,
      buyerPhone: this.state.buyer.phone,
      buyerEmail: this.state.buyer.email,

      bioAdaErr: !!(this.state.bioErr && Object.keys(this.state.bioErr).some(k => this.cekBiodata(this.state.buyer)[k])),
      bioErrMsg: (() => {
        const lama = this.state.bioErr || {}, kini = this.cekBiodata(this.state.buyer);
        const sisa = ["name", "phone", "email"].filter(k => lama[k] && kini[k]);
        if (!sisa.length) return "";
        return sisa.length > 1
          ? "Lengkapi dulu " + sisa.length + " data ini sebelum lanjut ya."
          : kini[sisa[0]];

      eName: this.warnaField("name"),
      ePhone: this.warnaField("phone"),

      rSheet: this.rSheet,
      sheetStart: this.sheetStart,
      sheetMove: this.sheetMove,
      sheetEnd: this.sheetEnd,
      rName: this.rBio.name,
      rPhone: this.rBio.phone,
      rEmail: this.rBio.email,

      onPhone: (e) => this.setBuyer("phone", e.target.value),
      onEmail: (e) => this.setBuyer("email", e.target.value),
      diagText: this.state.diag || "",
      diagOpen: typeof this.state.diag === "string",
      closeDiag: () => this.setState({ diag: undefined }),
      shipQ: this.state.ship.q,
      onShipQ: this.onShipQ,
      shipCari: this.state.ship.cari,
      shipHasil: this.state.ship.hasil.map(k => ({ label: k.label, pos: k.pos, pick: () => this.pilihKota(k) })),
      shipAdaHasil: this.state.ship.hasil.length > 0,
      shipAlamat: this.state.ship.alamat,
      onShipAlamat: e => {
        this.setShip({ alamat: e.target.value });
        if (e.target.value.trim().length > 6 && !this.state.ship.layanan.length && !this.state.ship.busy) this.cekOngkir(null);
      },
      shipFlat: !!this.state.ship.flat && !this.state.ship.layanan.length &&
        !this.state.ship.err && !this.state.ship.busy,
      shipBusy: this.state.ship.busy,
      shipErr: this.state.ship.err,
      shipAdaErr: !!this.state.ship.err,
      shipAdaLayanan: this.state.ship.layanan.length > 0,
      shipSisa: this.state.ship.layanan.length > 4 && !this.state.ship.semua,
      shipSisaLabel: "Lihat " + Math.max(0, this.state.ship.layanan.length - 4) + " layanan lain",
      shipLihatSemua: () => this.setShip({ semua: true }),
      shipLayanan: (this.state.ship.semua ? this.state.ship.layanan : this.state.ship.layanan.slice(0, 4)).map(s => {
        const aktif = this.state.ship.pilih === s;
        return {
          nama: s.kurir + " " + s.layanan,
          etd: "estimasi " + s.etd,
          biaya: s.biaya ? "Rp" + Number(s.biaya).toLocaleString("id-ID") : "GRATIS",
          border: aktif ? "#7E9B62" : "#E5EDD6",
          bg: aktif ? "#F3F7E9" : "#fff",
          weight: aktif ? "700" : "600",
          pick: () => this.setShip({ pilih: s })
        };

      shipSudahPilih: !!this.state.ship.pilih,
      shipBelumPilih: !this.state.ship.pilih,
      ongkirLabel: this.ongkirBiaya() ? "Rp" + this.ongkirBiaya().toLocaleString("id-ID") : "Gratis",

      payErrAda: !!this.state.payErr,

        "). Kami kirim link pembayarannya lewat WhatsApp.",
